UlSmallRBSpectralEffOptSw: Indicates whether to optimize the spectral efficiency of small RBs in the uplink. For FDD and TDD cells: If this option is selected, the spectral efficiency of small RBs in the uplink is optimized, which ensures that the transport block size calculated based on the optimized spectral efficiency is not less than the traffic volume to be scheduled. If this option is deselected, the spectral efficiency of small RBs in the uplink is not optimized. For NB-IoT cells: If this option is selected, the number of PUSCH RUs for scheduling is determined based on available resources in case that continuous PUSCH resources are insufficient in the time domain. If this option is deselected, the number of PUSCH RUs for scheduling is determined based on the data volume.

PuschUsePucchRbSwitch: Indicates whether PUCCH RBs can be occupied by the PUSCH. In scenarios with a single user: If this option is selected, PUCCH RBs can be occupied by the PUSCH; if this option is deselected, PUCCH RBs cannot be occupied by the PUSCH. In scenarios with multiple users, PUCCH RBs cannot be occupied by the PUSCH regardless of the setting of this option. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PuschDtxSchOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines whether to perform adaptive retransmission based on the PUSCH DTX detection result during uplink scheduling. This option takes effect only when uplink-downlink subframe configuration 2 or 5 is used. If an LTE TDD cell is established on an LBBPc, PUSCH DTX detection is not supported. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

PrachRbReuseSwitch: If this option is selected, the PUSCH and PRACH transmissions can use the same resource. If this option is deselected, the PUSCH and PRACH transmissions cannot use the same resource. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ULFSSAlgoswitch: If this option is deselected, uplink frequency-selective scheduling is disabled. If this option is selected, uplink frequency-selective scheduling is enabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

SrSchDataAdptSw: Indicates whether to enable data amount adaptation in SR-based scheduling. Data amount adaptation in SR-based scheduling is enabled only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

UlFssUserThdStSwitch: If this option is selected, the UE number threshold is optimized for uplink frequency selective scheduling. If this option is deselected, the UE number threshold is not optimized for uplink frequency selective scheduling. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HighOrderVMIMOSwitch: Indicates whether to enable high order MU-MIMO. If this option is selected, high order MU-MIMO is enabled. If this option is deselected, high order MU-MIMO is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

VMIMOReduceMCSRiseRBSwitch: Indicates whether to reduce the MCS index and increase the number of RBs for UEs to enable them to participate in pairing for MU-MIMO in non-massive-MIMO scenarios, or whether the beam determination criteria are loosened up for uplink MU-MIMO pairing in massive MIMO scenarios. In non-massive MIMO scenarios: If this option is selected, uplink MU-MIMO can be performed for UEs for which the MCS index is reduced and the number of RBs is increased. If this option is deselected, uplink MU-MIMO cannot be performed for UEs for which the MCS index is reduced and the number of RBs is increased. In massive MIMO scenarios: (1) If the option is selected, the eNodeB loosens up the beam determination criteria for uplink MU-MIMO pairing. (2) If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not loosen up the criteria. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

VoLTEUeVmimoSwitch: Indicates whether VoLTE UEs are allowed to participate in pairing for MU-MIMO. If this option is selected, VoLTE UEs are allowed to participate in pairing for MU-MIMO. If this option is deselected, VoLTE UEs are not allowed to participate in pairing for MU-MIMO. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

TtiBundlingForVideoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable TTI bundling for UEs running video services in weak coverage areas. If this option is selected, TTI bundling is enabled for UEs running video services in weak coverage areas, improving video service coverage at the cell edge. If this option is deselected, TTI bundling is disabled for UEs running video services in weak coverage areas. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: SpsSchSwitch(SpsSchSwitch), PreAllocationSwitch(PreAllocationSwitch), UlVmimoSwitch(UlVmimoSwitch), TtiBundlingSwitch(TtiBundlingSwitch), SmartPreAllocationSwitch(SmartPreAllocationSwitch), PuschDtxSwitch(PuschDtxSwitch), UlIblerAdjustSwitch(UlIblerAdjustSwitch), UlEnhancedFssSwitch(UlEnhancedFssSwitch), UlEnhancedSrSchSwitch(UlEnhancedSrSchSwitch), SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch(SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch), UlMinGbrSwitch(UlMinGbrSwitch), UlMbrCtrlSwitch(UlMbrCtrlSwitch), MbrUlSchSwitch(MbrUlSchSwitch), UeAmbrUlSchSwitch(UeAmbrUlSchSwitch), UlEnhancedDopplerSwitch(UlEnhancedDopplerSwitch), UlRaUserSchOptSw(UlRaUserSchOptSw), UlLast2RetransSchOptSwitch(UlLast2RetransSchOptSwitch), UlInterfFssSwitch(UlInterfFssSwitch), UlSmallRBSpectralEffOptSw(UlSmallRBSpectralEfficiencyOptSw), PuschUsePucchRbSwitch(PuschUsePucchRbSwitch), PuschDtxSchOptSwitch(PuschDtxSchOptSwitch), ULFSSAlgoSwitch(ULFSSAlgoSwitch), PrachRbReuseSwitch(PrachRbReuseSwitch), SrSchDataAdptSw(SrSchDataAdptSw), UlFssUserThdStSwitch(UlFssUserThdStSwitch), HighOrderVMIMOSwitch(HighOrderVMIMOSwitch), VMIMOReduceMCSRiseRBSwitch(VMIMOReduceMCSRiseRBSwitch), VoLTEUeVmimoSwitch(VoLTEUeVmimoSwitch), TtiBundlingForVideoSwitch(TtiBundlingForVideoSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: SpsSchSwitch, PreAllocationSwitch, UlVmimoSwitch, TtiBundlingSwitch, SmartPreAllocationSwitch, PuschDtxSwitch, UlIblerAdjustSwitch, UlEnhancedFssSwitch, UlEnhancedSrSchSwitch, SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch, UlMinGbrSwitch, UlMbrCtrlSwitch, MbrUlSchSwitch, UeAmbrUlSchSwitch, UlEnhancedDopplerSwitch, UlRaUserSchOptSw, UlLast2RetransSchOptSwitch, UlInterfFssSwitch, UlSmallRBSpectralEffOptSw, PuschUsePucchRbSwitch, PuschDtxSchOptSwitch, ULFSSAlgoSwitch, PrachRbReuseSwitch, SrSchDataAdptSw, UlFssUserThdStSwitch, HighOrderVMIMOSwitch, VMIMOReduceMCSRiseRBSwitch, VoLTEUeVmimoSwitch, TtiBundlingForVideoSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: SpsSchSwitch:Off, PreAllocationSwitch:On, UlVmimoSwitch:Off, TtiBundlingSwitch:Off, SmartPreAllocationSwitch:On, PuschDtxSwitch:On, UlIblerAdjustSwitch:Off, UlEnhancedFssSwitch:On

UlEnhancedSrSchSwitch: This option can be set by the eNodeB by default as follows:

On for FDD or TDD cells

Off for NB-IoT cells

SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch:Off, UlMinGbrSwitch:Off, UlMbrCtrlSwitch:Off, MbrUlSchSwitch:Off, UeAmbrUlSchSwitch:Off, UlEnhancedDopplerSwitch:On, UlRaUserSchOptSw:Off, UlLast2RetransSchOptSwitch:On, UlInterfFssSwitch:Off, UlSmallRBSpectralEffOptSw:Off, PuschUsePucchRbSwitch:Off, PuschDtxSchOptSwitch:Off, ULFSSAlgoSwitch:On, PrachRbReuseSwitch:Off, SrSchDataAdptSw:On, UlFssUserThdStSwitch:Off, HighOrderVMIMOSwitch:Off, VMIMOReduceMCSRiseRBSwitch:Off, VoLTEUeVmimoSwitch:Off, TtiBundlingForVideoSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • PsicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to On only if the UlVmimoSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, SpsSchSwitch of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Options UlPAMCSwitch of UlSchExtSwitch and UlIblerAdjustSwitch of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch cannot be selected simultaneously.
  • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the TtiBundlingSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in this MO cannot be both selected.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the TtiBundlingSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the TtiBundlingSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • If the NearBigPacketUeFastPcSwitch option of UlPcAlgoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: SmartPreAllocationSwitch: When DRX is disabled: (a) If this option is selected, the uplink delay increases, and the uplink interference is reduced, lowering UE power consumption. (b) If this option is deselected, the uplink delay decreases, and the uplink interference increases, raising UE power consumption. When DRX is enabled: (a) If this option is selected, the uplink delay decreases, and the downlink throughput increases. However, the uplink interference and UE power consumption increase. (b) If this option is deselected, the uplink delay increases, and the downlink throughput decreases. However, the uplink interference and UE power consumption decrease. The degree of impacts on network performance depends on the SmartPreAllocationDuration parameter value.

UlEnhancedFssSwitch: If this option is selected, the uplink MCS index and uplink throughput increase. If this option is deselected, the uplink MCS index and uplink throughput decrease.

UlEnhancedSrSchSwitch: For FDD and TDD cells, if uplink traffic is light in the cell and DRX is enabled, selecting this option decreases the BLERs of uplink initial transmission and retransmissions, reduces the waste of RBs and scheduling resources, mitigates interference to neighboring cells, and increases the cell throughput. However, uplink SR-based re-scheduling is performed only when the On Duration timer for the DRX long cycle starts. If normal SR-based scheduling fails, the ping delay increases. In scenarios where the uplink traffic volume is large or DRX is disabled, selecting this option has no impact on network performance. For NB-IoT cells, the attach success rate increases after this option is selected, and the attach success rate decreases after this option is deselected.

SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch: If this option is selected in a network with a medium or light load, the uplink transmit power increases by 0.2 dBm to 0.5 dBm on average, the uplink MCS index increases, and uplink throughput increases for UEs not in the cell center. The uplink throughput of UEs in the cell center does not change. If this option is selected in a network with a heavy load (for example, when the RB usage is greater than 70%), uplink interference slightly increases and the throughput of UEs exposed to uplink interference slightly decreases.

UlMinGbrSwitch: If this option is deselected, the uplink data rate of non-GBR services cannot be ensured. There is a possibility that no resource can be allocated to non-GBR services and service drop occurs. If this option is selected, the uplink data rates of non-GBR services are ensured based on the QCI-specific Min_GBR of these services, reducing the service drop probability. However, the cell capacity may decrease, as the eNodeB attempts to ensure the Min_GBR of cell-edge UEs.

UlMbrCtrlSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB allocates more uplink resources to UEs for which the MBR is greater than the GBR and GBR bearers are set up. This increases the uplink traffic volume when uplink resources are sufficient. If the MBR is equal to the GBR for UEs, selecting this option has no impacts on the network performance. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB performs scheduling based only on the GBR set for uplink GBR bearers and network performance is not affected.

MbrDlSchSwitch/UeAmbrDlSchSwitch: If this option is selected, throughput of UEs with higher MBR/UE-AMBR increases, and throughput of UEs with lower MBR/UE-AMBR decreases. In addition, the system throughput may also be affected. The impact on the system throughput depends on the difference in MBR/UE-AMBR between UEs and the distribution of UEs in the cell. If this option is deselected, the throughput differences between UEs with different MBRs/UE-AMBRs decrease. In addition, the system throughput is not affected by the difference in MBR/UE-AMBR between UEs or the distribution of UEs in the cell.

UlEnhancedDopplerSwitch: If this option is selected, the performance of low-speed UEs improves. The cell coverage and user-perceived rate of CEUs increase, and the PRB usage decreases.

UlRaUserSchOptSw: If this option is selected, the scheduling priority of access signaling is increased, improving the network access performance (denoted by KPIs such as the RRC connection setup success rate and average RRC connection setup delay). However, if a large number of UEs access the network at the same time and congestion occurs during signaling scheduling, uplink data transmission will be affected, causing uplink throughput to decrease. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

UlLast2RetransSchOptSwitch: Indicates whether to optimize the scheduling policy for the last two retransmissions. If this option is selected, the scheduling policy is optimized for the last two retransmissions. If this option is deselected, the default scheduling policy is used.

Deselecting this option does not affect network performance.

Selecting this option decreases the RBLER in the uplink in most cases. However, the downlink cell throughput decreases when a large amount of PDCCH resources are consumed for retransmissions. In addition, the uplink cell throughput decreases and the scheduling delay for UEs not at the cell edge increases if excessive PDCCH resources or RBs are consumed because the last two retransmissions need to be performed for a large number of UEs.

UlInterfFssSwitch: Selecting this option decreases the uplink RBLER. Other network KPIs are not affected.

Deselecting this option has no impact on network performance.

UlSmallRBSpectralEffOptSw: For NB-IoT cells: (a) If this option is selected, the PUSCH scheduling success rate increases and the number of resource fragments decreases. (b) If this option is deselected, the PUSCH scheduling success rate decreases and the number of resource fragments increases.

PuschUsePucchRbSwitch: The number of activated L2 UEs in each TTI is used to determine whether the cell is serving a single UE. If the number of activated L2 UEs is 1, the cell is serving a single UE. In this case, if this option is selected, PUCCH RBs can be occupied by the PUSCH, the peak rate of UEs in the cell center increases, but interference to the PUCCH may increase. If the number of activated L2 UEs is greater than 1, this option does not take effect, and the network performance is not affected.

UlIblerAdjustSwitch: If this option is selected, the uplink throughput in benchmark drive tests increases. In live networks, if there are large-packet services in the uplink, selecting this option increases uplink throughput (especially at the cell edge) and slightly increases the RBLER. If there are no large-packet services in the uplink in live networks, selecting this option has no impact on network KPIs.

PuschDtxSchOptSwitch: If this option is selected, uplink scheduling resources can be saved and the cell throughput increases. If this option is deselected, the network performance is not affected.

PrachRbReuseSwitch: If this option is selected, the uplink data rate increases but the RRC connection setup success rate may decrease. If this option is deselected, the RRC connection setup success rate is not affected but the uplink data rate decreases.

ULFSSAlgoswitch: If this option is selected, uplink frequency-selective scheduling takes effect when the network load is light, which improves the uplink MCS index and increases throughput, and uplink frequency-selective scheduling does not take effect when the network load is heavy, which reduces the number of resource fragments and increases throughput. If this option is deselected, uplink frequency-selective scheduling is disabled. The uplink MCS index and throughput decrease when the network load is light.

SrSchDataAdptSw: If this option is deselected, data amount adaptation in SR-based scheduling is disabled, and ping delay may increase.

If this option is selected (the default setting), data amount adaption in SR-based scheduling is enabled, and the network performance is not affected.

UlFssUserThdStSwitch: If this option is selected, the proportion of UEs that are selected for uplink frequency selective scheduling increases, uplink user data rate increases, and uplink RBLER decreases.

If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

HighOrderVMIMOSwitch: If this option is selected, a maximum of four UEs can use the same time-frequency resource to transmit data, which increases the overall uplink throughput of the cell.

If this option is deselected, four UEs cannot use the same time-frequency resource to transmit data, which does not increase the overall uplink throughput of the cell.

VMIMOReduceMCSRiseRBSwitch: If this option is selected for a non-massive-MIMO cell, the MCS index can be reduced to increase the number of RBs for UEs to be paired for MU-MIMO, which increases the uplink cell throughput. If this option is selected for a massive MIMO cell, uplink beam evaluation for MU-MIMO pairing is less strict, further increasing the average number of layers paired in the uplink and improving the uplink cell throughput. However, the uplink BLER in the cell also increases.

If this option is deselected for a non-massive-MIMO cell, the MCS index cannot be reduced to increase the number of RBs for UEs to be paired for MU-MIMO, which decreases the uplink cell throughput. If this option is deselected for a massive MIMO cell, uplink beam evaluation for MU-MIMO pairing is remain unchanged. As a result, the average number of layers paired in the uplink, uplink cell throughput, and the uplink BLER in the cell remain unchanged.

VoLTEUeVmimoSwitch: If this option is selected, VoLTE UEs can be paired for MU-MIMO, which increases the number of VoLTE UEs supported in the cell.

If this option is deselected, VoLTE UEs cannot be paired for MU-MIMO, which decreases the number of VoLTE UEs supported in the cell.

TtiBundlingForVideoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable TTI bundling for UEs running video services in weak coverage areas. If this option is selected, TTI bundling is enabled for UEs running video services in weak coverage areas, improving video service coverage at the cell edge. If this option is deselected, TTI bundling is disabled for UEs running video services in weak coverage areas.

Applicable Mode: L
DacqEnhancementSwitch DACQ Enhancement Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable dynamic AMBR control by QCI (DACQ) enhancement functions.

EnhancementCtrlSwitch: Indicates whether to enable enhanced DACQ. If this option is selected, enhanced DACQ is enabled. If this option is deselected, enhanced DACQ is disabled. This option can be selected only if the DacqSwitch parameter is set to ON. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CaUserLimitOptSwitch: If this option is deselected, DACQ is performed to restrict the overall rate of each CA UE identified as a rate-restricted UE. If this option is selected, DACQ is performed to restrict the rate of CA UEs identified as rate-restricted UEs on specific carriers. Before selecting this option, select the EnhancementCtrlSwitch option. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: EnhancementCtrlSwitch(EnhancementCtrlSwitch), CaUserLimitOptSwitch(CaUserLimitOptSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: EnhancementCtrlSwitch, CaUserLimitOptSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: EnhancementCtrlSwitch:Off, CaUserLimitOptSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • If the EnhancementCtrlSwitch option of the DacqEnhancementSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the DacqSwitch parameter in the same MO must be set to ON.
  • The EnhancementCtrlSwitch option of the DacqEnhancementSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected when the CaUserLimitOptSwitch option of the DacqEnhancementSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: EnhancementCtrlSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB performs differentiated rate control for different UEs or multi-level rate control, increasing the accuracy of DACQ and improving user experience.

CaUserLimitOptSwitch: No impact.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
RanShareModeSwitch RAN Sharing Mode Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether admission control, congestion control, and scheduling policies are operator-specific. If this parameter is set to ON, admission control, congestion control, and scheduling policy implementation are operator-specific, and the unused RBs of an operator can be used by other operators. On FDD and TDD networks, operators share data channel resources according to the specified RB percentages. On NB-IoT networks, operators share UE context storage resources according to the specified percentages. If this parameter is set to STATIC_SHARING, admission control, congestion control, and scheduling policy implementation are operator-specific, and the unused RBs of an operator cannot be used by other operators. If this parameter is set to OFF, admission control, congestion control, and scheduling policy implementation are not operator-specific.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On), STATIC_SHARING(STATIC_SHARING)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON, STATIC_SHARING

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • When the OpPrbGroupShareSwitch option of the OpResourceGroupShareSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the RanShareModeSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can only be set to OFF.

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L
FreqPriorityHoSwitch Based on Frequency Priority HO Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handover algorithms. This parameter includes the following options:

FreqPriorIFHOSwitch: Indicates whether frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers can be triggered. Select this option if a frequency is dedicated for carrying services. If this option is selected, frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers are triggered even when the serving cell has good signal quality. If this option is deselected, frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers cannot be triggered, and inter-frequency handovers can be triggered only when other conditions for triggering handovers are met. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

FreqPriorIFBlindHOSwitch: If this option is selected, frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers are performed in a blind manner. If this option is deselected, frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers are performed based on measurements. This option takes effect only when the FreqPriorIFHOSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MlbBasedFreqPriHoSwitch: Indicates whether frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers can be triggered during MLB. If this option is selected, the eNodeB stops performing frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers for UEs that newly access the cell during MLB. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB continues performing frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers for UEs that newly access the cell during MLB. This option takes effect only when the FreqPriorIFHOSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

A2BasedFreqPriHoSwitch: Indicates whether measurements for frequency-priority-based handovers are triggered based on event A2. If this option is selected, new UEs (including UEs that newly access the cell, handed over to the cell, and reestablish RRC connections in the cell) that meet the conditions for triggering event A2 perform measurements on high-priority frequencies. If this option is deselected, new UEs that meet conditions for triggering event A1 perform measurements on high-priority frequencies or perform blind handovers. This option takes effect only when the FreqPriorIFHOSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

LoadTriggerFreqPriHoSwitch: Indicates whether to trigger frequency-priority-based handovers based on the load of the target cell. If this option is selected, the serving cell obtains loads of candidate cells. If there is a candidate cell for which user-number-based MLB has not been triggered, this cell can be selected as the target cell for frequency-priority-based handovers from the serving cell. If user-number-based MLB has been triggered for all candidate cells, frequency-priority-based handovers cannot be triggered for UEs in the serving cell. If the option is deselected, the serving cell does not consider loads of candidate cells when determining whether to trigger frequency-priority-based handovers. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: FreqPriorIFHOSwitch(FreqPriorIFHOSwitch), FreqPriorIFBlindHOSwitch(FreqPriorIFBlindHOSwitch), MlbBasedFreqPriHoSwitch(MlbBasedFreqPriHoSwitch), A2BasedFreqPriHoSwitch(A2BasedFreqPriHoSwitch), LoadTriggerFreqPriHoSwitch(LoadTriggerFreqPriHoSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: FreqPriorIFHOSwitch, FreqPriorIFBlindHOSwitch, MlbBasedFreqPriHoSwitch, A2BasedFreqPriHoSwitch, LoadTriggerFreqPriHoSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: FreqPriorIFHOSwitch:Off, FreqPriorIFBlindHOSwitch:On, MlbBasedFreqPriHoSwitch:Off, A2BasedFreqPriHoSwitch:Off, LoadTriggerFreqPriHoSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: FreqPriorIFHOSwitch: If this option is selected, frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers can be performed for UEs. The number of handovers increases, and the service rate increases.

FreqPriorIFBlindHOSwitch: If this option is selected, the number of gap-assisted measurements decreases. To ensure the handover success rate, strictly set the trigger condition of the frequency-priority-based inter-frequency handovers (that is, setting the threshold related to event A1 to a large value).

MlbBasedFreqPriHoSwitch: If this option is selected, MLB is triggered, and the target frequency for frequency-priority-based handovers is the frequency used to offload traffic of the source cell during MLB, then the loads in the network can be centrally managed based on the MLB algorithms when the network is heavily loaded, thereby improving resource usage. If this option is deselected, the target frequency selected based on target frequency configuration in a frequency-priority-based handover is different from the target frequency selected for load balancing, thereby improving resource usage.

A2BasedFreqPriHoSwitch: Selecting this option applies when cells working at high-priority frequencies and cells working at low-priority frequencies are served by different eNodeBs. Deselecting this option applies when cells working at high-priority frequencies and cells working at low-priority frequencies are served by the same eNodeB.

LoadTriggerFreqPriHoSwitch: If this option is selected and the target cell for frequency-priority-based handovers is enabled with MLB, the serving cell determines whether to trigger frequency-priority-based handovers to the target cell based on the fact that whether UE-number-based MLB has been triggered in the target cell. In this way, the number of invalid gap-assisted measurements decreases, handover preparation failures can be avoided, average user throughput increases, and signaling overheads related to load exchange over the X2 interfaces increase. Deselecting this option will generate a large number of invalid gap-assisted measurements and cause a large number of handover preparation failures, but does not increase signaling overheads related to load exchange over the X2 interfaces.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
MuBfAlgoSwitch MUBF Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable multi-user beamforming (MU beamforming). This parameter is valid only if the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter is selected.

MuBfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MU beamforming in the downlink for a cell. If this option is selected, MU beamforming in the downlink is enabled for a cell (or an SFN physical cell). If this option is deselected, MU beamforming in the downlink is disabled for a cell (or an SFN physical cell). This option applies only to LTE TDD.

TM9MuBfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MU beamforming in the downlink for closed-loop TM9 UEs in a cell. The setting of this option takes effect only when the MuBfSwitch option of this parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected. If this option is selected, the MuBfSwitch option of this parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected, and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected, MU beamforming in the downlink is enabled for closed-loop TM9 UEs in a cell. If this option is deselected, MU beamforming in the downlink is disabled for closed-loop TM9 UEs in a cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

MubfResAdjSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MU beamforming resource adjustment. The setting of this option takes effect only when the MuBfSwitch option of this parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected. If this option is selected and the MuBfSwitch option of this parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected, downlink resource adjustment for MU beamforming is enabled for the cell. If this option is deselected, downlink resource adjustment for MU beamforming is disabled for the cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

HarqRetranPairSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MU beamforming for UEs subjected to HARQ retransmissions. This option is valid only if the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter is selected. If both the MuBfSwitch option and this option are selected, MU beamforming is enabled for UEs subjected to HARQ retransmissions in the cell. If this option is deselected, MU beamforming is disabled for UEs subjected to HARQ retransmissions in the cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

SplitSdmaSwitch: Indicates whether to enable split SDMA for massive MIMO cells. If this option is selected, adaptation between MU beamforming and split SDMA for the PDSCH is supported. If this option is deselected, split SDMA for the PDSCH is not supported. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

TurboBfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable turbo beamforming in WTTx scenarios. If both the MuBfSwitch and TurboBfSwitch options are selected, turbo beamforming takes effect in 8T8R normal cells.

TM9SplitSdmaSwitch: Indicates whether downlink closed-loop TM9 UEs can participate in split SDMA. When the TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch and SplitSdmaSwitch options are selected: (1) If this option is selected, closed-loop TM9 UEs can participate in split SDMA. (2) If this option is deselected, closed-loop TM9 UEs cannot participate in split SDMA. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: MuBfSwitch(MuBfSwitch), TM9MuBfSwitch(TM9MuBfSwitch), MubfResAdjSwitch(MubfResAdjSwitch), HarqRetranPairSwitch(HarqRetranPairSwitch), SplitSdmaSwitch(SplitSdmaSwitch), TurboBfSwitch(TurboBfSwitch), TM9SplitSdmaSwitch(TM9SplitSdmaSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: MuBfSwitch, TM9MuBfSwitch, MubfResAdjSwitch, HarqRetranPairSwitch, SplitSdmaSwitch, TurboBfSwitch, TM9SplitSdmaSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: MuBfSwitch:Off, TM9MuBfSwitch:Off, MubfResAdjSwitch:Off, HarqRetranPairSwitch:Off, SplitSdmaSwitch:Off, TurboBfSwitch:Off, TM9SplitSdmaSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the TM9MuBfSwitch option can be selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter only if the MuBfSwitch option is selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option is selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the MubfResAdjSwitch option can be selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter only if either of the following conditions is met: The MuBfSwitch option is selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option is selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter.
  • If DMIMOJTSwitch of DMIMOAlgoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, MuBfSwitch of MuBfAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • The MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected if the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • When the NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch option of the NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: The TM9MuBfSwitch and MubfResAdjSwitch options can be selected only when the MuBfSwitch option of this parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected. The HarqRetranPairSwitch option can be selected only when the MuBfSwitch option is selected.

If the MuBfSwitch option of this parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected, MU beamforming is enabled. The pairing can be performed between a maximum of two layers when LBBPd boards are used or four layers when UBBP boards are used, which increases the average downlink cell throughput in heavy-load scenarios.

MubfResAdjSwitch: It is recommended that this option be selected. If this option is selected, MU beamforming can be used to adjust resources in light-load scenarios, reducing the interference of the cell to neighboring cells or the number of residual small packets generated when two UEs are paired for MU beamforming.

TM9MuBfSwitch: If this option is selected, MU beamforming is enabled for closed-loop TM9 UEs in a heavily loaded cell, increasing the average downlink cell throughput. Do not select this option if a cell is lightly loaded or MU beamforming is not required for closed-loop TM9 UEs in a cell. It is recommended that the TM9MuBfSwitch option be deselected because MU beamforming for closed-loop TM9 UEs has poorer performance than that for TM8 or open-loop TM9 UEs.

HarqRetranPairSwitch: If this option is selected, MU beamforming is enabled for UEs subjected to HARQ retransmissions, increasing the average downlink cell throughput. However, the downlink BLER may increase. If this option is deselected, MU beamforming is disabled for UEs subjected to HARQ retransmissions, decreasing the gains in the average downlink cell throughput, but stabilizing the downlink BLER.

SplitSdmaSwitch: If this option is selected, split SDMA for the PDSCH is supported, improving spectral efficiency and the user-perceived rate. If this option is deselected, expected gains cannot be obtained.

TurboBfSwitch: If this option is selected, the MU beamforming algorithm in the turbo beamforming feature can be optimized in WTTx scenarios, increasing the number of pairing layers and spectral efficiency. If this option is deselected, the MU beamforming algorithm in the turbo beamforming feature cannot be optimized in WTTx scenarios. In this case, the number of pairing layers and spectral efficiency do not increase, and expected performance gains cannot be achieved.

TM9SplitSdmaSwitch: If this option is selected, closed-loop TM9 UEs can participate in split SDMA to obtain TM9 multi-stream pairing gains. However, some TM7 UEs cannot participate in pairing due to beam conflicts. If this option is deselected, no TM9 multi-stream pairing gains can be produced.

Applicable Mode: L
DistBasedHoSwitch Distance-based Handover Switch

Meaning: Indicates the switch used to enable or disable distance-based handovers. If this switch is turned on, distance-based handovers are allowed. If this switch is turned off, distance-based handovers are prohibited. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this switch is turned on, distance-based handovers are allowed and the handover success rate increases. If this switch is turned off, distance-based handovers are prohibited and the handover success rate decreases.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
AcBarAlgoSwitch AcBar algorithms switch

Meaning: Indicates the switch for the access barring algorithm. If this parameter is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC, dynamic access barring is enabled. In this situation, the access probability factors for mobile-originated data and mobile-originated signaling are dynamically adjusted based on the cell load. If this parameter is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_STATIC, static access barring is enabled. In this situation, the access probability factors and average access barring durations are configured in the access barring information. If this parameter is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DISABLE, access barring is disabled. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC(dynamic configuration), ACBAR_SWITCH_STATIC(static configuration), ACBAR_SWITCH_DISABLE(Disable)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC, ACBAR_SWITCH_STATIC, ACBAR_SWITCH_DISABLE

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: ACBAR_SWITCH_STATIC(static configuration)

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if AcBarAlgoSwitch is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC, either the DYNAMIC_FOR_MO or DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC option of AcBarAlgoforDynSwitch must be selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC, the access probability factors for mobile-originated calls and mobile-originated signaling are dynamically adjusted based on the current cell load. In this way, the probabilities of UEs accessing the cell can be controlled to relieve the cell load.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
SfnUlSchSwitch SFN UL Scheduling Switch

Meaning: Indicates the SFN uplink scheduling mode. The uplink scheduling modes include the joint scheduling mode, and the auto-selection mode between joint scheduling and independent scheduling. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: ADAPTIVE(Adaptive scheduling), JOINT(Joint scheduling)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: ADAPTIVE, JOINT

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: FDD: SFN: JOINT(Joint scheduling); TDD: Indoor SFN: JOINT(Joint scheduling), Outdoor SFN: ADAPTIVE(Adaptive scheduling), Cell combination: JOINT(Joint scheduling).

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SfnUlSchSwitch is set to ADAPTIVE or SfnDlSchSwitch is set to ADAPTIVE, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch must be deselected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If the cell load is high, adaptive scheduling between joint scheduling and independent scheduling is used to improve the total throughput of the cell.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
SfnDlSchSwitch SFN DL Scheduling Switch

Meaning: Indicates the SFN downlink scheduling mode. The downlink scheduling modes include the joint scheduling mode, and the auto-selection mode between joint scheduling and independent scheduling. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: ADAPTIVE(Adaptive scheduling), JOINT(Joint scheduling)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: ADAPTIVE, JOINT

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: FDD: SFN: JOINT(Joint scheduling); TDD: Indoor SFN: JOINT(Joint scheduling), Outdoor SFN: ADAPTIVE(Adaptive scheduling), Cell combination: JOINT(Joint scheduling).

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In a CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SfnDlSchSwitch is not set to ADAPTIVE, then the SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SfnUlSchSwitch is set to ADAPTIVE or SfnDlSchSwitch is set to ADAPTIVE, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch must be deselected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If the cell load is high, adaptive scheduling between joint scheduling and independent scheduling is used to improve the total throughput of the cell.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
IrcSwitch IRC Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to apply MRC/IRC adaptation to the PUCCH/PRACH in the cell.

PucchIrcSwitch: If this option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation applies to the PUCCH. If this option is deselected, MRC applies to the PUCCH. The LBBPc does not support PUCCH MRC/IRC adaptation. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PrachIrcSwitch: If the eNodeB is equipped with LBBPc boards and uses one or two RX antennas, PrachIrcSwitch of the IrcSwitch parameter and the PrachIntrfRejSwitch parameter together control the interference rejection technique applied to the PRACH.

If the PrachIntrfRejSwitch parameter is set to OFF, interference rejection is not applied to the PRACH. When the PrachIntrfRejSwitch parameter is set to ON, PrachIrcSwitch of the IrcSwitch parameter takes effect. If the PrachIrcSwitch option is deselected, MRC is applied to the PRACH. If the PrachIrcSwitch option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is applied to the PRACH.

If the eNodeB is equipped with LBBPc boards and uses more than two RX antennas, interference rejection is not supported.

If the eNodeB is equipped with LBBPd, UBBPd, or UBBPe boards, the PrachIntrfRejSwitch parameter setting does not take effect. In this case, if the PrachIrcSwitch option is deselected, MRC is applied to the PRACH; if the PrachIrcSwitch option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is applied to the PRACH. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the false detection probability decreases and the detection performance deteriorates. If this option is deselected, the false detection probability increases and the detection performance improves. This option takes effect only when the PucchIrcSwitch option is selected. This option takes effect only in 8T8R and massive MIMO scenarios. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: PucchIrcSwitch, PrachIrcSwitch, PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch(PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: PucchIrcSwitch, PrachIrcSwitch, PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: PucchIrcSwitch:Off, PrachIrcSwitch:Off, PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If the PrachIrcSwitch option of this parameter is selected, the PRACH false detection probability decreases, the success rates of access, handovers, resynchronization, and RRC connection reestablishments through the RACH increase. However, the probability that UEs in weak coverage areas attempt to access the network increases, thereby decreasing the RRC connection establishment success rate.

If the PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch option is selected, the false detection probability decreases and the detection performance deteriorates. If the PucchFalseAlarmOptSwitch option is deselected, the false detection probability increases and the detection performance improves.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
DynDrxSwitch Dynamic DRX switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable dynamic DRX. DynDrxSwitch: If this option is selected, dynamic DRX is used for newly admitted UEs to reduce signaling overhead or decrease UE power consumption. If this option is deselected, dynamic DRX is not used for newly admitted UEs. Dynamic DRX can be used for CA UEs only when this option is selected for not only the PCell but also the SCells. If the DynDrxSwitch option is selected, setting the DrxAlgSwitch parameter to ON cannot ensure that DRX is enabled for synchronized UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: DynDrxSwitch(DynDrxSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: DynDrxSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: DynDrxSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: When this switch is on, the dynamic DRX feature reduces the signaling overhead and decreases UE power consumption, achieving a trade-off between the signaling overhead and UE power consumption.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
HighMobiTrigIdleModeSwitch High Mobility Triggered Idle Mode Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the high-mobility-triggered-idle switch. When this parameter is set to ENABLE, UEs in high mobility are released and enter the idle mode, and therefore the signaling impact on the network caused by frequent handovers are reduced. When this parameter is set to DISABLED, UEs in high mobility are not released. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: DISABLE(Disable), ENABLE(Enable)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: DISABLE, ENABLE

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: DISABLE(Disable)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
AvoidInterfSwitch Interference avoidance switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable interference avoidance in the cell.

AvoidGeranInterfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the GSM and LTE buffer zone optimization feature. If this option is selected, the feature is enabled.

Before selecting this option, pay attention to the following:

1. Change the uplink and downlink bandwidths of the LTE cell in advance to the immediate higher level of standard bandwidth. For example, if the current uplink and downlink bandwidths of the LTE cell are 15 MHz, change them to 20 MHz.

2. It is recommended that this option be deselected if the cell bandwidth is 1.4 MHz, 3 MHz, 5 MHz, or 10 MHz.

3. To avoid co-channel interference from GERAN cells to the PUCCH, select the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter.

TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UpPTS interference detection and avoidance. If this option is selected, UpPTS interference detection and avoidance are enabled for LTE TDD.

AvoidPrachInterfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PRACH interference avoidance in the frequency domain. If this option is selected, PRACH interference avoidance in the frequency domain is enabled for LTE TDD.

AvoidUtranInterfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the UMTS and LTE buffer zone optimization feature. If this option is selected, the feature is enabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch: Indicates whether to enable in-band interference monitoring. In-band interference monitoring is enabled for the cell only if this option is selected. The setting of this option does not take effect for LTE TDD cells established on LBBPc boards or LTE FDD cells established on LBBPc and LBBPd boards.

AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable spurious interference avoidance. If this option is selected, spurious interference avoidance is enabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

UlInterfSuppressionSwitch: Indicates whether to automatically adjust uplink power control parameters based on interference and perform conservative scheduling for access signaling. If this option is selected, uplink power control parameters are automatically adjusted based on interference, and conservative scheduling is performed for access signaling. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the CDMA and LTE zero buffer zone feature. It is recommended that this option be selected at the buffer zone of CDMA and LTE refarming. Because of frequency band differences between RATs, the CDMA and LTE zero buffer zone feature is mutually exclusive with the GL DSS, GSM and LTE zero buffer zone, and UMTS and LTE buffer zone optimization features. You are advised not to enable CDMA and LTE zero buffer zone with GL DSS, GSM and LTE zero buffer zone, or UMTS and LTE buffer zone optimization. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

AvoidCrsConflictInterfSw: Indicates whether to enable interference avoidance optimization when CRS conflicts occur due to inter-cell D-MIMO. If this option is selected, interference avoidance optimization takes effect when CRS conflicts occur. If this option is deselected, interference avoidance optimization does not take effect when CRS conflicts occur. This option applies only to LTE TDD. To deploy inter-cell D-MIMO, PCIs must be replanned, which causes interference due to CRS conflicts. It is recommended that this option be selected if inter-cell D-MIMO causes CRS conflicts and be deselected in other CRS conflict scenarios.

Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch: Indicates whether to enable interference coordination for cells with uplink-downlink subframe configuration 1 or 2. Interference coordination is enabled for such cells only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: AvoidGeranInterfSwitch(AvoidGeranInterfSwitch), TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch(TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch), AvoidPrachInterfSwitch(AvoidPrachInterfSwitch), AvoidUtranInterfSwitch(AvoidUtranInterfSwitch), IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch(IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch), AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch(AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch), UlInterfSuppressionSwitch(UlInterfSuppressionSwitch), AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch(AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch), AvoidCrsConflictInterfSw(AvoidCrsConflictInterfSw), Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch(Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: AvoidGeranInterfSwitch, TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch, AvoidPrachInterfSwitch, AvoidUtranInterfSwitch, IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch, AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch, UlInterfSuppressionSwitch, AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch, AvoidCrsConflictInterfSw, Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: AvoidGeranInterfSwitch:Off

TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch:Off

AvoidPrachInterfSwitch:Off

AvoidUtranInterfSwitch:Off

IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch:Off

AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch:Off

UlInterfSuppressionSwitch:Off

AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch:Off

AvoidCrsConflictInterfSw:Off

Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch is selected, EicicSwitch must be set to OFF.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch is selected, FeicicSwitch must be set to OFF.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: AvoidGeranInterfSwitch: If this option is deselected, the LTE spectrum does not overlap with the GSM spectrum and accordingly network KPIs are not affected. If this option is selected, the available bandwidth of the E-UTRAN cell increases. In this situation, more RBs can be dynamically used to improve the average cell throughput based on whether UEs can resist GERAN interference. However, because the LTE spectrum may overlap with the GSM spectrum, the access success rate and handover success rate may drop, and the service drop rate may increase.

TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch: If this option is deselected, downlink beamforming does not take effect for CEUs when UpPTS interference is severe. If this option is selected, the eNodeB adjusts the SRSs originally transmitted on the UpPTS to common uplink subframes after severe UpPTS interference occurs, and downlink beamforming becomes effective for CEUs. After SRSs are adjusted to common uplink subframes, the last symbol in each uplink subframe cannot be used to transmit the PUSCH. Only 10 instead of 11 symbols are used to transfer data in each uplink subframe after SRSs are adjusted to the uplink subframe. If the number of RBs used by a UE and the MCS index remain unchanged, the uplink throughput of the UE decreases by 9% under the worst conditions.

AvoidPrachInterfSwitch: If this option is deselected, PRACH modulation quality is poor if severe PRACH interference occurs, and access to the cell becomes difficult for UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB moves the PRACH to a frequency domain position with weak interference if severe PRACH interference occurs, improving PRACH modulation quality and UE access experience.

AvoidUtranInterfSwitch: If this option is deselected, LTE uplink scheduling may cause strong interference to UMTS intra-frequency neighboring cells in the uplink. If this option is selected, the eNodeB restricts uplink RB scheduling to reduce interference to UMTS intra-frequency neighboring cells, which reduces LTE uplink capacity.

AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch: If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform differentiated UE scheduling on uplink RBs with severe interference, lowering the throughput of CEUs that are scheduled on such RBs, access success rate, and handover success rate while increasing the service drop rate. If this option is selected, the eNodeB schedules only UEs between the cell center and edge or not at the cell edge on uplink RBs with severe interference. In this case, the access success rate and handover success rate increase, and the service drop rate decreases, but LTE uplink capacity is affected.

UlInterfSuppressionSwitch: If this option is selected, KPIs such as the service drop rate and the handover success rate are optimized when uplink interference exceeds the value of the UlInterfsuppressionThd parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO.

AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB restricts uplink and downlink RB scheduling to alleviate interference to CDMA intra-frequency neighboring cells, reducing LTE uplink and downlink capacity. If this option is deselected, LTE uplink and downlink scheduling may cause strong interference to CDMA intra-frequency neighboring cells in the uplink and downlink.

TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB automatically reestablishes cells when interference occurs or required signal quality recovers from deterioration due to interference.

AvoidCrsConflictInterfSw: If this option is selected, interference between cells with CRS conflicts decreases, improving the experience of CEUs in the cells. If this option is deselected, no optimization is performed to avoid inter-cell interference to cells when CRS conflicts occur, and the interference causes lowering of the MCS index and deterioration of user experience.

IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB checks whether there is in-band interference in uplink muted subframes (there is one muted radio frame each 512 radio frames), and the uplink throughput decreases by a maximum of 0.2%.

Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch: During key event assurance on a network consisting of cells with different uplink-downlink configurations, selecting this option will increase the uplink and downlink throughput of cells with uplink-downlink configuration 1 and decrease the uplink and downlink throughput of cells with uplink-downlink configuration 2, and deselecting this option has no impact on network performance. Any modification to the setting of this option will lead to an automatic cell reset.

Applicable Mode: L
GLPwrShare GL power share switch

Meaning: Indicates the switch used to enable or disable the GL dynamic power sharing algorithm. This algorithm can be used when the GSM and LTE carriers share the same power amplifier. This improves power usage and service quality of LTE UEs adopting quadrature phase shift keying (QPSK) as their modulation scheme in case that the GSM and LTE busy hours appear at different time points or that the traffic is unbalanced. This parameter indicates the switch for the GL dynamic power sharing feature at the LTE side. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • The DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if GLPwrShare of the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch is selected, GLPwrShare must be set to OFF.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
EicicSwitch Eicic Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to disable the TDM-based eICIC algorithm or apply the static or dynamic mode to the eICIC algorithm. In a heterogeneous network (HetNet), the eICIC function is used together with the cell range expansion (CRE) function of the micro base station to expand the coverage and increase the number of users absorbed by the micro cell. To implement the eICIC function, ensure that the value of this parameter must be set consistently for macro and micro cells. Static mode applies only to TDD, and dynamic mode applies only to FDD. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), STATIC(Static), DYNAMIC(Dynamic)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, STATIC, DYNAMIC

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to STATIC or DYNAMIC when OutOfBandRelaySwitch under the RelaySwitch parameter is turned on.
  • The following conditions are mutually exclusive with each other: The EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to STATIC or DYNAMIC; the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter is selected.
  • The following conditions are mutually exclusive with each other: The EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to STATIC or DYNAMIC; the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter is selected.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if FeicicSwitch is set to ABS, EicicSwitch must be set to DYNAMIC.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch is selected, EicicSwitch must be set to OFF.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to OFF, TDM-based eICIC is disabled. The number of UEs absorbed by the micro cell is small, and users have different user experience in the macro cell and in the micro cell. If this parameter is set to STATIC or DYNAMIC, TDM-based eICIC is enabled. In this case, if a CRE is not configured, throughput in the macro cell decreases. If a proper CRE is configured, the number of UE handovers from the macro cell to the micro cell increases, the number of UEs absorbed by the micro cell increases, user experience in the macro cell and that in the micro cell becomes almost the same, and throughput of network edge UEs increases. In a HetNet, dynamic eICIC provides higher cell edge user throughput in a more stable manner than static eICIC.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
PUSCHMaxRBPUCCHAdjSwitch PUCCH Resource Switch Based on UL Peak Rate

Meaning: Indicates whether RBs are allocated to the PUCCH based on the uplink peak rate. If this parameter is set to ON(On), PUCCH resources are flexibly allocated based on the principle that fewer RBs are allocated to the PUCCH when there are only a few UEs. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If there are only a few UEs, setting this parameter to ON improves the uplink throughput but does not ensure the BHCA specifications of admitted UEs. As a result, the RRC connection setup success rate may decrease during busy hours.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
DlCompSwitch Downlink CoMP algorithms switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the DL CoMP algorithm.

If the IntraDlCompSwitch option is selected and the InterDlCompDcsSwitch and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options are deselected, intra-BBP adaptive DL CoMP is enabled, and the eNodeB adaptively selects DCS, CBF, or JT as the transmission mode.

If the IntraDlCompSwitch and InterDlCompDcsSwitch options are selected and the InterDlCompCbfSwitch option is deselected, intra-BBP adaptive DL CoMP, inter-BBP DCS, and inter-eNodeB DCS are enabled.

If the IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options are selected, intra-BBP adaptive DL CoMP, inter-BBP adaptive DL CoMP, and inter-eNodeB adaptive DL CoMP are enabled. In inter-BBP adaptive DL CoMP and inter-eNodeB adaptive DL CoMP, the eNodeB adaptively selects DCS or CBF as the transmission mode.

The IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options apply only to LTE TDD.

FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch: Indicates whether to enable DL CoMP in a homogeneous network (consisting of only macro or micro cells) under a Cloud BB architecture. DL CoMP is enabled in such a network only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch: Indicates whether to enable DL CoMP in a heterogeneous network (consisting of macro and micro cells) under a Cloud BB architecture. DL CoMP is enabled in such a network only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Tm9JtSwitch: Indicates whether to enable JT in TM9 in FDD networks. JT in TM9 is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

IntraDlCompJtSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the intra-eNodeB SU-JT algorithm. Intra-BBP SU-JT is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: IntraDlCompSwitch(IntraDlCompSwitch), InterDlCompDcsSwitch(InterDlCompDcsSwitch), InterDlCompCbfSwitch(InterDlCompCbfSwitch), FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch(FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch), FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch(FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch), Tm9JtSwitch(Tm9JtSwitch), IntraDlCompJtSwitch(IntraDlCompJtSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, InterDlCompCbfSwitch, FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch, FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch, Tm9JtSwitch, IntraDlCompJtSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: IntraDlCompSwitch:Off,

InterDlCompDcsSwitch:Off,

InterDlCompCbfSwitch:Off,

FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch:Off,

FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch:Off,

Tm9JtSwitch:On,

IntraDlCompJtSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • If the InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter is selected, the InterDlCompDcsSwitch option cannot be deselected.
  • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch under the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to 1, IntraDlCompSwitch under the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to 1.
  • If AdptCellEdgePwrAllocSw of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch of DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • The SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch, FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch, and Tm9JtSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected if the IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, or InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If the IntraDlCompSwitch option is selected, the throughput of CEUs increases, but the average cell throughput may decrease.

If the InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch option is selected, the throughput of CEUs in cells in an inter-eNodeB cluster increases, but the average cell throughput may decrease.

If the Tm9JtSwitch option is selected, the throughput of CEUs and the average cell throughput increase.

If the DL CoMP feature is disabled, the throughput of CEUs decreases due to co-channel interference.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
PsicSwitch PSIC algorithm switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable parallel soft interference cancellation (PSIC). In the case of LTE FDD, PSIC can be used in cells established on UBBP or LBBPd boards in uplink 2x2 MU-MIMO scenarios. In the case of LTE TDD, PSIC can be used in cells established on UBBP boards in uplink 2x4 MU-MIMO scenarios. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: MumimoPuschPsicSwitch(MumimoPuschPsicSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: MumimoPuschPsicSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: MumimoPuschPsicSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • PsicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to On only if the UlVmimoSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the MumimoPuschPsicSwitch option of the PsicSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the MumimoPuschPsicSwitch option of the PsicSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: For FDD, if the switch is turned on and there are UEs paired for 2x2 MU-MIMO, cell throughput increases.

For TDD, if the switch is turned on and there are UEs paired for 2x4 MU-MIMO, cell throughput increases.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
MlbHoMode Load Balancing Handover Policy

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable or disable handovers triggered by mobility load balancing (MLB), based on which the eNodeB determines handover policies.

InterFreqMlbBlindHo: If this option is selected, the eNodeB preferentially performs a blind handover to an intra-RAT inter-frequency neighboring cell for load balancing. Otherwise, the eNodeB does not perform the blind handover. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterRatMlbBlindHo: If this option is selected, the eNodeB preferentially performs a blind handover from an E-UTRAN cell to a UTRAN or GERAN cell for load balancing. Otherwise, the eNodeB does not perform the blind handover. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterFreqMlbRedirect: If this option is selected, the eNodeB is allowed to perform a load-based redirection to an intra-RAT inter-frequency neighboring cell. Otherwise, the eNodeB is not allowed to perform the redirection. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterRatMlbRedirect: If this option is selected, the eNodeB is allowed to perform a redirection from an E-UTRAN cell to a UTRAN or GERAN cell. Otherwise, the eNodeB is not allowed to perform the redirection. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: InterFreqMlbBlindHo(InterFreqMlbBlindHo), InterRatMlbBlindHo(InterRatMlbBlindHo), InterFreqMlbRedirect(InterFreqMlbRedirect), InterRatMlbRedirect(InterRatMlbRedirect)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: InterFreqMlbBlindHo, InterRatMlbBlindHo, InterFreqMlbRedirect, InterRatMlbRedirect

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: InterFreqMlbBlindHo:Off, InterRatMlbBlindHo:Off, InterFreqMlbRedirect:Off, InterRatMlbRedirect:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: InterFreqMlbBlindHo: If this option is selected, inter-frequency blind handovers are triggered for load balancing. This reduces the signaling overheads for gap-assisted measurements but decreases the handover success rate when blind-handover neighboring cells are not properly configured. If this option is deselected, gap-measurement-based inter-frequency handovers are triggered for load balancing. This increases the signaling overheads for gap-assisted measurements but ensures the handover success rate.

InterRatMlbBlindHo: If this option is selected, inter-RAT blind handovers are triggered for load balancing. This reduces the signaling overheads for gap-assisted measurements but decreases the handover success rate when blind-handover neighboring cells are not properly configured. If this option is deselected, gap-measurement-based inter-RAT handovers are triggered for load balancing. This increases the signaling overheads for gap-assisted measurements but ensures the handover success rate.

InterFreqMlbRedirect: If this option is selected, UEs that do not support inter-frequency handovers can perform inter-frequency load balancing based on inter-frequency redirections. If this option is deselected, UEs that do not support inter-frequency handovers cannot perform inter-frequency load balancing.

InterRatMlbRedirect: If this option is selected, UEs that do not support inter-RAT handovers can perform inter-RAT load balancing based on inter-RAT redirections. If this option is deselected, UEs that do not support inter-RAT handovers cannot perform inter-RAT load balancing.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
UplinkCompSwitch Uplink Comp Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable uplink coordinated multipoint reception (UL CoMP) for a cell.

UlJointReceptionSwitch: Indicates whether to enable intra-eNodeB intra-BBP UL CoMP. Intra-eNodeB intra-BBP UL CoMP is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch: Indicates whether to enable intra-eNodeB inter-BBP UL CoMP. Intra-eNodeB inter-BBP UL CoMP is enabled only if this option is selected. This option can be selected only when the UlJointReceptionSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlJointReception3CellSwitch: Indicates whether to enable 3-cell UL CoMP in the serving cell. The coordinating cell cannot determine whether it is involved in three-cell UL CoMP. If cell A's UlJointReception3CellSwitch is on and cell B's UlJointReceptionSwitch is also on, then 3-cell UL CoMP is enabled in cell A, regardless of whether UlJointReception3CellSwitch is on in cell B (the coordinated cell). Three-cell UL CoMP is enabled in the serving cell only if this option is selected. This option can be selected only when the UlJointReceptionSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL CoMP in HetNet. UL CoMP is enabled in HetNet only if this option is selected. This option can be selected only when the UlJointReceptionSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlHetnetCompManualNcellCfgSw: Indicates whether to enable manual configuration of a macro cell's neighboring cell list for measurement and a micro cell's homing macro cell. If this option is deselected, automatic configuration is enabled. If this option is selected, manual configuration is enabled. One micro cell can belong to only one macro cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw: Indicates whether to use UL RSRP measurement for selecting coordinating cells. If this option is deselected, A3-based measurement is used for selecting coordinating cells in macro-micro scenarios. If this option is selected, A3-based measurement is used in macro-macro scenarios, and UL RSRP or A3-based measurement is used in macro-micro scenarios. This option takes effect only when the UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

SimplePairedCompSw: Indicates whether to select UL CoMP UEs for VMIMIO pairing. If this option and the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of this parameter and the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter are selected, UL CoMP UEs in cells working in 8R mode can be selected for MU-MIMO pairing. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

VoipPrioSw: Indicates whether to prioritize VoIP UEs. If this option is selected, VoIP UEs have a higher priority than other UEs. If this option is deselected, VoIP UEs have a lower priority than other UEs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlCompInterBoardType2Sw: Indicates whether to enable inter-BBP UL CoMP for type-2 UEs. If this option and the UlJointReceptionSwitch and UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch options are selected, inter-BBP UL CoMP for type-2 UEs is enabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

UlCompForVideoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL CoMP for uplink video services. If this option is selected, UL CoMP can be enabled exclusively for UEs running video services. If this option is deselected, UL CoMP cannot be enabled exclusively for UEs running video services. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

WideRangeCompSwitch: Indicates whether to perform UL CoMP in a wider scope. If this option is selected, the eNodeB performs UL CoMP in a wider scope. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB cannot perform UL CoMP in a wider scope. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

EnhancedUlCompSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the enhanced UL CoMP algorithm, which allows UL CoMP to take effect in a wider range. The enhanced UL CoMP algorithm takes effect only after this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: UlJointReceptionSwitch(UlJointReceptionSwitch), UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch(UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch), UlJointReception3CellSwitch(UlJointReception3CellSwitch), UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch(UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch), UlHetnetCompManualNcellCfgSw(UlHetnetCompManualNcellCfgSw), UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw(UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw), SimplePairedCompSw(SimplePairedCompSw), VoipPrioSw(VoipPrioSw), UlCompInterBoardType2Sw(UlCompInterBoardType2Sw), UlCompForVideoSwitch(UlCompForVideoSwitch), WideRangeCompSwitch(WideRangeCompSwitch), EnhancedUlCompSwitch(EnhancedUlCompSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: UlJointReceptionSwitch, UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch, UlJointReception3CellSwitch, UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch, UlHetnetCompManualNcellCfgSw, UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw, SimplePairedCompSw, VoipPrioSw, UlCompInterBoardType2Sw, UlCompForVideoSwitch, WideRangeCompSwitch, EnhancedUlCompSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: UlJointReceptionSwitch:Off, UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch:Off, UlJointReception3CellSwitch:Off, UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch:Off, UlHetnetCompManualNcellCfgSw:Off, UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw:Off, SimplePairedCompSw:Off, VoipPrioSw:Off, UlCompInterBoardType2Sw:Off, UlCompForVideoSwitch:Off, WideRangeCompSwitch:Off, EnhancedUlCompSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch under UplinkCompSwitch is turned on, UlJointReceptionSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch must also be turned on.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if UlJointReception3CellSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch is turned on, UlJointReceptionSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch must also be turned on.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch, if UlCompInterBoardType2Sw under UplinkCompSwitch is turned on, UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch under UplinkCompSwitch must also be turned on.
  • The UlHighOrderCompfor2R option of the UlHighOrderCompSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the UlJointReception3CellSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter or the SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The UlHighOrderCompfor2R option of the UlHighOrderCompSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: UlJointReceptionSwitch: If this option is selected, the average uplink throughput of cells and the throughput of CEUs increase. The improved uplink throughput of CEUs indicates better uplink coverage.

UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch: If this option is selected, inter-BBP UL CoMP is enabled, supporting more UL CoMP UEs and having better performance than when intra-BBP UL CoMP is enabled.

UlJointReception3CellSwitch: If this option is deselected, 3-cell UL CoMP is disabled. If this option is selected, 3-cell UL CoMP is enabled, with better performance than 2-cell UL CoMP.

UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch: If this option is selected, the uplink throughput of macro and micro cells increase.

UlHetnetCompManualNcellCfgSw: The setting of this option has no impact on network performance.

UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw: If this option is selected, the uplink throughput of macro-cell CEUs close to the micro cell increases.

SimplePairedCompSw: If this option is selected, UL CoMP UEs can be selected for MU-MIMO, UL CoMP performance improves, and the UE pairing rate increases. If this option is deselected, UL CoMP UEs cannot be selected for MU-MIMO.

VoipPrioSw: If this option is selected, VoIP UEs take priority over other UEs.

UlCompInterBoardType2Sw: If this option is selected, and there are inter-BBP intra-frequency neighboring cells, interference between CEUs in these cells is mitigated, improving the average cell throughput. If this option is deselected, the function does not take effect.

UlCompForVideoSwitch: If this option is selected, UL CoMP can be enabled exclusively for UEs running video services. In this case, the uplink throughput of CEUs running video services in the cell increases, fewer CEUs running video services are restricted in the uplink, and the video experience of such UEs improves.
