WideRangeCompSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB performs UL CoMP in a wider scope, increasing the uplink throughput. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB cannot perform UL CoMP in a wider scope. In this case, the uplink throughput does not increase.

EnhancedUlCompSwitch: If this option is selected, the proportion of UEs for which UL CoMP takes effect increases, increasing the uplink throughput. If this option is deselected, the proportion of UEs for which UL CoMP takes effect and the uplink throughput do not increase.

Applicable Mode: L
AntCalibrationAlgoSwitch Antenna calibration algorithm switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the antenna calibration algorithm. This parameter applies only to TDD cells. CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch: If this option is selected, virtual antenna mapping (VAM) performance is improved for two groups of 4T cross-polarized antennas. If this option is deselected, the antenna calibration algorithm is disabled. It is recommended that this option be selected when there are two CRS ports and two groups of 4T cross-polarized antennas are used. You are advised to deselect this option in other scenarios. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch(CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: It is recommended that this switch be on for two groups of 4T cross-polarized antennas.

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this switch is on, the downlink cell throughput increases.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
DynSpectrumShareSwitch Dynamic Spectrum Share Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable dynamic spectrum sharing in the E-UTRAN cell, including GSM and LTE dynamic spectrum sharing and GL zero bufferzone.

GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch: The GSM and LTE Dynamic Spectrum Sharing feature is enabled only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch: The GSM and LTE Zero Bufferzone feature is enabled only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Before the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch or GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch option is selected, parameters HighFreqShareRbNum and LowFreqShareRbNum must be set. By means of spectrum sharing, idle GERAN spectrum can be used by the E-UTRAN cell, increasing the bandwidth and throughput of the E-UTRAN cell.

GUI Value Range: GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch(GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch), GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch(GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch, GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: None

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch: If this option is selected, the E-UTRAN cell can use more spectrum resources, and therefore the E-UTRAN capacity is increased.

GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch: If this option is selected, the E-UTRAN cell in the GL bufferzone can use the spectrum overlap between the GSM and LTE, and therefore the E-UTRAN capacity is increased. If this option is deselected, the E-UTRAN cell in the GL bufferzone cannot use the spectrum overlap between the GSM and LTE.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD
SfnLoadBasedAdptSwitch SFN Load-based Adaptive Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable load-based adaptive adjustment in the adaptive SFN/SDMA feature. If this parameter is set to ON(On), load-based adaptive adjustment is enabled, which increases the proportion of joint-scheduling UEs and improves user experience when the network load is light, and increases the proportion of independent-scheduling UEs and ensures the spectral efficiency when the network load is heavy. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), load-based adaptive adjustment in the adaptive SFN/SDMA feature cannot be enabled. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • The SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch option cannot be selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO if the SfnLoadBasedAdptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch is set to OFF.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON(On), load-based adaptive adjustment is enabled, which increases the proportion of joint-scheduling UEs and improves user experience when the network load is light, and increases the proportion of independent-scheduling UEs and ensures the spectral efficiency when the network load is heavy. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), load-based adaptive adjustment in the adaptive SFN/SDMA feature cannot be enabled.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
PuschIrcAlgoSwitch PUSCH IRC algorithm switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to apply the interference rejection combining (IRC) algorithms to the PUSCH. This parameter includes the MrcIrcAdptSwitch, SmartIrcSwitch, MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R, and NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch options. The SmartIrcSwitch option applies only to 8R and 4R CoMP and Massive MIMO scenarios. The NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch option applies only to 8R scenarios. The SmartIRCSwitch and NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch options apply only to LTE TDD macro cells.

MrcIrcAdptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MRC/IRC adaptation. When this option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is enabled. When this option is deselected, MRC is used.

SmartIrcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable smart IRC. Smart IRC is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R: Indicates whether to enable MRC/IRC adaptation in uplink 2R scenarios. This option takes effect only when the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is selected. When this option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is enabled in 2R scenarios. When this option is deselected, MRC is used. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch: Indicates whether to enable enhanced smart IRC for scenarios without interference. Enhanced no-interference IRC is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

In 2R cells established on LBBPc boards: If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is selected, IRC is used. If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is deselected, MRC is used. In 2R cells established on LBBPd boards: The setting of MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R takes effect only when the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is selected. If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch and MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R options are selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is used. If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is selected and the MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R option is deselected, IRC is used. If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is deselected, MRC is used. In 2R cells established on the UBBP boards: If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is used. If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is deselected, MRC is used. In 4R cells established on the LBBPc, LBBPd, or UBBP boards: If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is used. If the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option is deselected, MRC is used.

GUI Value Range: MrcIrcAdptSwitch(MrcIrcAdptSwitch), SmartIrcSwitch(SmartIrcSwitch), MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R(MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R), NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch(NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: MrcIrcAdptSwitch, SmartIrcSwitch, MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R, NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: MrcIrcAdptSwitch: This option can be set to the following default values:

FDD or TDD cells: On

NB-IoT cells: Off

SmartIrcSwitch:Off, MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R:Off, NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SmartIrcSwitch under PuschIrcAlgoSwitch is turned on, MrcIrcAdptSwitch under PuschIrcAlgoSwitch must also be turned on.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: MrcIrcAdptSwitch: If this option is selected, both the uplink coverage and the uplink throughput of CEUs in interference scenarios increase. If this option is deselected, the uplink coverage decreases in interference scenarios. SmartIrcSwitch: If this option is selected, both the uplink average throughput and throughput at the cell edge increase. In addition, the service drop rate decreases. If this option is deselected, both the uplink average throughput and throughput at the cell edge decrease in interference scenarios. In addition, the service drop rate increases. MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R: This option takes effect for 2-antenna reception only when the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option of this parameter is selected. If the MrcIrcAdaptivefor2R option is selected, MRC/IRC adaptation is used for 2-antenna reception; if this option is deselected, IRC is used for 2-antenna reception. If MRC/IRC adaptation is used for 2-antenna reception, the uplink coverage improves in no-interference scenarios and deteriorates in strong interference scenarios, as compared with fixed IRC.NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch: If this option is selected, interference suppression gains can be obtained and network performance in no-interference scenarios improves. If this option is deselected, smart IRC is used and only interference suppression gains can be obtained.

Applicable Mode: L
AsAlgoSwitch Accelerate Service Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable network service acceleration guarantee over the air interface. If this parameter is set to ON(ON), network service acceleration guarantee-related functions and parameters for acceleration-involved UEs take effect. If this parameter is set to OFF(OFF), network service acceleration guarantee-related functions and parameters for acceleration-involved UEs do not take effect. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(OFF), ON(ON)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(OFF)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON, the uplink and downlink rates of acceleration-involved UEs increase over the air interface, improving user experience of services such as web browsing. If this parameter is set to OFF, the rates of UEs in the cell are not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
ReselecPriAdaptSwitch Reselection Priority Adaptive Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable adaptive dedicated cell reselection priority. If this switch is turned on, the eNodeB delivers the dedicated cell reselection priority to a UE when the following conditions are met: (1) The UE requests to set up an RRC connection with the cause value being mo-Signalling. (2) The MME instructs the eNodeB to release the S1 bearer. (3) The eNodeB fails to obtain the UE capability. If this switch is turned off, the eNodeB does not deliver dedicated cell reselection priority to a UE even if the eNodeB fails to obtain the UE capability information. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
AnrFunctionSwitch ANR Function Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether intra-RAT or inter-RAT neighbor relationship optimization takes effect.

INTRA_RAT_ANR_SW: Intra-RAT neighbor relationship optimization takes effect only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

INTER_RAT_ANR_SW: Fast ANR with NG-RAN and optimization of inter-RAT neighbor relationships with UTRAN, GERAN, and CDMA2000 cells take effect only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NR_EVENT_ANR_SW: If this option is selected, event-triggered ANR with NG-RAN is enabled. This type of ANR creates and optimizes inter-RAT neighbor relationships with NG-RAN cells as triggered by coverage-based, service-based, or DC-based NG-RAN measurements. If this option is deselected, inter-RAT neighbor relationships with NG-RAN cells are not created or optimized based on measurements. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: INTRA_RAT_ANR_SW(INTRA_RAT_ANR_SW), INTER_RAT_ANR_SW(INTER_RAT_ANR_SW), NR_EVENT_ANR_SW(NR_EVENT_ANR_SW)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: INTRA_RAT_ANR_SW, INTER_RAT_ANR_SW, NR_EVENT_ANR_SW

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: None

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
SfnAlgoSwitch SFN Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable algorithms related to single frequency networks (SFNs).

SfnPdcchDcsSwitch: Indicates whether to enable selective transmission of PDCCH signals for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, selective transmission of PDCCH signals is performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, joint transmission or SDMA-based transmission of PDCCH signals is performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch: Indicates whether to enable selective reception of L2 data for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, one of the working RRUs serving a UE is selected to receive L2 data of this UE.

If this option is deselected, only the target RRU of a UE is fixedly used to receive L2 data of this UE.

SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch: Indicates whether to enable SDMA-based transmission of PDCCH signals for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, SDMA-based transmission of PDCCH signals is performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, joint transmission or selective transmission of PDCCH signals is performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnPucchAckSdmaSwitch: Indicates whether to enable SDMA-based transmission and reception of dynamic ACK over the PUCCH for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, SDMA-based transmission and reception of dynamic ACK over the PUCCH can be performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, SDMA-based transmission and reception of dynamic ACK over the PUCCH cannot be performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SuperCombCellSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the super combined cell feature. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, the super combined cell feature is enabled.

If this option is deselected, the super combined cell feature is disabled.

SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable downlink two-load-level adaptation for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, downlink two-load-level adaptation is enabled when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, downlink two-load-level adaptation is disabled when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch: Indicates whether to adjust downlink scheduling attributes for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, downlink scheduling attributes are adjusted when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, downlink scheduling attributes are not adjusted when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnCbfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable coordinated beamforming (CBF) for an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, CBF is performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, CBF is not performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnVoLteDlJSchSwitch: Indicates whether to jointly schedule VoLTE UEs in the downlink in an adaptive SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, VoLTE UEs are always jointly scheduled when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, VoLTE UEs are adaptively scheduled when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch: Indicates whether to enable adaptive use of uplink SINR and uplink RSRP for target RRU selection. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, the eNodeB chooses either the uplink SINR or the uplink RSRP based on the UE location for target RRU selection.

If this option is deselected, the eNodeB always uses the uplink SINR for target RRU selection.

SfnVoLteUlJSchSwitch: Indicates whether to jointly schedule VoLTE UEs in the uplink in an adaptive SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, VoLTE UEs are always jointly scheduled in the uplink when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

If this option is deselected, VoLTE UEs are adaptively scheduled in the uplink when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnDlRblerOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the optimization to resolve consecutive downlink bit errors. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, the optimization is enabled to resolve consecutive downlink bit errors or MCS index reduction to 0.

If this option is deselected, the optimization is disabled.

SfnJtSwitch: Indicates whether to enable joint transmission for TM9/TM10 UEs in the overlapping areas of an SFN cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If this option is selected, the SFN joint transmission function is enabled.

If this option is deselected, the SFN joint transmission function is disabled.

AsfnFastAttSwitch: Indicates whether to enable fast UE scheduling attribute decision for adaptive SFN. This option applies only to FDD.

If this option is selected, fast UE scheduling attribute decision is enabled for adaptive SFN, and TM9 can be used in an adaptive SFN cell served by a maximum of six RRUs.

If this option is deselected, fast UE scheduling attribute decision is disabled for adaptive SFN, and TM9 can be used in an adaptive SFN cell served by only two RRUs.

GUI Value Range: SfnPdcchDcsSwitch(SFNPDCCHDCSSWITCH), SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch(SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch), SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch(SFNPDCCHSDMASWITCH), SfnPucchAckSdmaSwitch(SFNPUCCHACKSDMASWITCH), SuperCombCellSwitch(SuperCombCellSwitch), SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch(SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch), SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch(SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch), SfnCbfSwitch(SfnCbfSwitch), SfnVoLteDlJSchSwitch(SfnVoLteDlJSchSwitch), SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch(SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch), SfnVoLteUlJSchSwitch(SfnVoLteUlJSchSwitch), SfnDlRblerOptSwitch(SfnDlRblerOptSwitch), SfnJtSwitch(SfnJtSwitch), AsfnFastAttSwitch(AsfnFastAttSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: SfnPdcchDcsSwitch, SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch, SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch, SfnPucchAckSdmaSwitch, SuperCombCellSwitch, SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch, SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch, SfnCbfSwitch, SfnVoLteDlJSchSwitch, SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch, SfnVoLteUlJSchSwitch, SfnDlRblerOptSwitch, SfnJtSwitch, AsfnFastAttSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: SfnPdcchDcsSwitch:Off, SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch:Off, SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch:Off, SfnPucchAckSdmaSwitch:Off, SuperCombCellSwitch:Off, SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch:Off, SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch:Off, SfnCbfSwitch:Off, SfnVoLteDlJSchSwitch:Off, SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch:Off, SfnVoLteUlJSchSwitch:Off, SfnDlRblerOptSwitch:Off, SfnJtSwitch:Off, AsfnFastAttSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, SfnPdcchDcsSwitch and SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch cannot be both selected.
  • The SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • In a CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SfnDlSchSwitch is not set to ADAPTIVE, then the SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SfnUlSchSwitch is set to ADAPTIVE or SfnDlSchSwitch is set to ADAPTIVE, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch must be deselected.
  • If either PucchSwitch or PucchFlexCfgSwitch of PucchAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • If the PucchMeasOptSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in this MO must also be selected.
  • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, SpsSchSwitch of DlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, SpsSchSwitch of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • If the SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be selected.
  • The SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch option cannot be selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO if the SfnLoadBasedAdptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch is set to OFF.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the TM9MuBfSwitch option can be selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter only if the MuBfSwitch option is selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option is selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the MubfResAdjSwitch option can be selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter only if either of the following conditions is met: The MuBfSwitch option is selected under the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter or the SfnCbfSwitch option is selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter.
  • The SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch, FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch, and Tm9JtSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: SfnPdcchDcsSwitch:

If this option is selected, PDCCH coverage and capacity improve when the PDCCH is heavily loaded and suffers severe interference.

If this option is deselected, PDCCH reliability can be ensured when the PDCCH is lightly loaded.

SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch:

If this option is selected, uplink coverage improves.

If this option is deselected, only the target RRU of a UE is fixedly used to receive L2 data of this UE.

SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch:

If this option is selected, the eNodeB uses SDMA-based transmission to improve PDCCH capacity.

If this option is deselected, joint transmission or selective transmission of PDCCH signals is performed when adaptive SFN/SDMA is enabled.

SfnPucchAckSdmaSwitch:

If this option is selected, the SDMA-based PDCCH transmission efficiency increases, but the dynamic ACK detection performance deteriorates.

If this option is deselected, the dynamic ACK detection performance improves, but the SDMA-based PDCCH transmission efficiency decreases.

SuperCombCellSwitch:

If this option is selected, handovers between SFN cells in a super combined cell are not perceivable, improving user experience and increasing the average throughput of CEUs in SFN cells.

If this option is deselected, the super combined cell feature does not take effect, and the common SFN networking mode is used.

SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch:

If this option is selected, two-load-level adaptation is enabled, making SFN more stable.

If this option is deselected, load levels are not distinguished.

SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch:

If this option is selected, user-perceived rates increase.

If this option is deselected, scheduling is not optimized.

SfnCbfSwitch:

If this option is selected, inter-RRU CBF reduces interference and increases the cell capacity.

If this option is deselected, inter-RRU CBF is disabled.

SfnVoLteDlJSchSwitch:

If this option is selected, joint scheduling is enabled for VoLTE UEs, improving user experience.

If this option is deselected, adaptive scheduling is enabled for VoLTE UEs, increasing the cell capacity but degrading user experience.

SfnTarRruAdptSelSwitch:

When this option is selected: (1) If the timing offset of received uplink signals is large, the result of SINR measurement at a non-target RRU is inaccurate. In this case, RSRP is used to select a target RRU, ensuring correct selection of a target RRU. (2) If the timing offset of received uplink signals is small, uplink SINR is used to select a target RRU.

When this option is deselected, uplink SINR is always used to select a target RRU. If the timing offset of received uplink signals is small, correct selection of a target RRU can be ensured. If the timing offset of received uplink signals is large, correct selection of a target RRU cannot be ensured.

SfnVoLteUlJSchSwitch:

If this option is selected, uplink joint scheduling is enabled for VoLTE UEs, improving uplink voice service experience of these UEs.

If this option is deselected, uplink adaptive scheduling is enabled for VoLTE UEs, increasing the uplink cell capacity but degrading uplink voice service experience of these UEs.

SfnDlRblerOptSwitch:

If this option is selected, the optimization is enabled to resolve consecutive downlink bit errors or MCS index reduction to 0, lowering the RBLER.

If this option is deselected, the optimization is disabled. In this case, the RBLER may increase.

SfnJtSwitch:

If this option is selected, multi-stream SDM can be used for TM9 and TM10 UEs in the overlapping areas of an SFN cell, increasing the user-perceived data rates of these UEs. The scheduling performance in CSI-RS subframes is poor for non-TM9 and non-TM10 UEs, which causes the average user-perceived data rates to decrease. If the penetration rate of TM9 and TM10 UEs is low and CSI-RS is configured for the cell, selecting this option negatively affects the average cell throughput and user-perceived data rates.

If this option is deselected, SFN joint transmission does not take effect, and the setting does not affect the SFN network performance.

AsfnFastAttSwitch:

If this option is selected, UE scheduling attributes are adjusted in each TTI based on service loads, which improves the performance of UEs involved in space division multiplexing in the adaptive SFN cell and increases user-perceived rates.

If this option is deselected, fast UE scheduling attribute decision does not take effect, and the setting does not affect the performance of the adaptive SFN cell.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
PrachIntrfRejSwitch Prach Interference Rejection Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to suppress interference on the PRACH. This parameter takes effect only on the eNodeB using the LBBPc and one or two RX antennas. If this parameter is set to OFF, the interference on the PRACH is not suppressed. If this parameter is set to ON, the PrachIrcSwitch option of the IrcSwitch parameter takes effect. If the PrachIrcSwitch option of the IrcSwitch parameter is deselected, interference on the PRACH is suppressed based on the maximum ratio combining (MRC). If the PrachIrcSwitch option of the IrcSwitch parameter is selected, interference on the PRACH is suppressed based on the MRC or the interference rejection combining (IRC). This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to OFF, the number of false alarms increases in scenarios of strong interference on the PRACH. If this parameter is set to ON, the number of false alarms in the PRACH decreases, random access success rate, handover success rate, resynchronization rate, and RRC reestablishment success rate increase. However, UEs in areas with weak coverage may attempt to access the network, which decreases the RRC setup success rate.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
EnhMIMOSwitch Enhanced MIMO Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the algorithms related to enhanced MIMO.

TM9Switch: Indicates whether to allow UEs to enter TM9. UEs are allowed to enter TM9 only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch: Indicates whether to allow UEs meeting certain conditions to adaptively enter TM4 and TM9. UEs are allowed to adaptively enter TM4 and TM9 only if this option is selected. The TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch option can be selected only after the TM9Switch option is selected. TM4 and TM9 adaptation does not work in cells established on LBBPc boards. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Disuse statement: This option will be deleted in a later version according the N+2 principle. Although the configuration interface in this version supports delivery of the setting of this option, the system does not use this option. Therefore, avoid using this option.

TM10Switch: Indicates whether to allow UEs to enter TM10. UEs are allowed to enter TM10 only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

ChannelRecSwitch: Indicates whether to enable channel reconstruction. Channel reconstruction is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the TM9 hybrid precoding algorithm in massive MIMO scenarios. If this option is selected, the TM9 hybrid precoding algorithm is enabled to improve the beamforming capability of closed-loop TM9 UEs. If this option is deselected, the TM9 hybrid precoding function is disabled, and the beamforming capability of closed-loop TM9 UEs remains unchanged. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch: Indicates whether to enable 4-port TM9 enhancement in 8T scenarios. If this option is selected, the enhancement is enabled. The eNodeB sets appropriate precoded 4-port CSI-RS beams for UEs based on SRSs, allowing an 8-port beamforming capability for 4-port TM9 UEs. If this option is deselected, the enhancement is disabled. The eNodeB does not configure TM9 for 4-port TM9 UEs in 8T scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: TM9Switch(TM9Switch), TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch(TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch), TM10Switch(TM10Switch), ChannelRecSwitch(ChannelRecSwitch), TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch(TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch), 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch(8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: TM9Switch, TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch, TM10Switch, ChannelRecSwitch, TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch, 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: TM9Switch:Off, TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch:Off, TM10Switch:Off, ChannelRecSwitch:Off, TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch:Off, 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • Options TM10Switch and TM9Switch of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch cannot be selected simultaneously.
  • If the TM9Pusch12Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is selected, the ChannelRecSwitch option of the same parameter must be deselected.
  • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, options of TM9Switch and TM10Switch of EnhMIMOSwitch must be deselected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: TM9Switch: The setting of this option determines the transmission modes that the eNodeB with multiple antennas can select for the UEs served by this eNodeB. When this option is selected and there are TM9 UEs on the network, the average cell throughput increases. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

The TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch option is valid when the TM9 switch is on, and its setting determines the transmission modes that the eNodeB with multiple antennas can select for TM9 UEs. The specific impact of this option on throughput and coverage performance is as follows:

(1) If this option is deselected, the transmission modes of all TM9 UEs in the cell are TM9. For its impact, see the description of TM9Switch.

(2) If this option is selected, the transmission modes of TM9 UEs of category 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12, or 13 in the cell can only be TM4 or TM9, and the peak rates of these TM9 UEs increase.

TM10Switch: The setting of this option determines the transmission modes that the eNodeB with multiple antennas can select for the UEs served by this eNodeB. When this option is selected and there are TM10 UEs on the network, the average cell throughput increases. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

ChannelRecSwitch: When this option is selected, the downlink channel reconstruction accuracy increases, improving the experience and throughput performance of TM9 UEs. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected. For a massive MIMO cell where DDB (32T32R) is enabled, enabling channel reconstruction decreases throughput when the number of UEs is small.

TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch: When this option is selected, the experience and throughput performance of TM9 UEs improve. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

8T4portTM9EnhanceSwitch: When this option is selected, the experience and throughput of 4-port TM9 UEs improve in 8T scenarios. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
InterfRandSwitch Interference Randomize Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates the interference randomization algorithm applied to TDD and FDD eNodeBs.

If this parameter is set to OFF, the interference randomization algorithm is disabled.

If this parameter is set to ECO_BASED, the eCoordinator-based interference randomization algorithm is enabled. This algorithm applies only to TDD eNodeBs.

If this parameter is set to ENB_BASED, the eNodeB-based interference randomization algorithm is enabled. This algorithm applies to both TDD and FDD eNodeBs. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(OFF), ECO_BASED(ECO_BASED), ENB_BASED(ENB_BASED)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ECO_BASED, ENB_BASED

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(OFF)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: This parameter applies to lightly-loaded networks. If this parameter is set to ENB_BASED, interference is reduced in a lightly-loaded cell and the spectrum efficiency is improved, and the MCS is more evenly distributed. If this parameter is set to ECO_BASED, the eCO-based interference randomization is enabled and the cell spectrum efficiency is improved.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
RepeaterSwitch Repeater Switch

Meaning: Indicates the type of reference signal (RS) power to be delivered in system information block type 2 (SIB2). If the AntRsPwrSwitch option is selected, the RS power calculated based on the AntOutputPwr parameter value is delivered in SIB2. If the AntRsPwrSwitch option is deselected, the RS power specified by the ReferenceSignalPwr parameter is delivered in SIB2.

GUI Value Range: AntRsPwrSwitch(AntRsPwrSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: AntRsPwrSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: AntRsPwrSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L
MultiFreqPriControlSwitch Multi Frequency Priority Control Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to prioritize frequencies in a multi-frequency network.

FixedMeasObjIDSwitch: Indicates whether the eNodeB maps each neighboring frequency to a MeasObjectID based on the frequency measurement priorities. If this option is selected, the eNodeB maps each neighboring frequency to a corresponding MeasObjectID based on the frequency measurement priorities. A higher-priority frequency is mapped to a smaller MeasObjectID. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not map neighboring frequencies to MeasObjectIDs based on the frequency measurement priorities. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoipMeasFreqPriSwitch: Indicates whether the frequency priorities specified by the VoipPriority parameter take effect for coverage-based handovers of VoIP UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB includes only intra-RAT neighboring frequencies with the highest priority based on the settings of the VoipPriority parameter when the eNodeB delivers measurement configurations to VoIP UEs for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not consider the settings of the VoipPriority parameter when delivering measurement configurations to VoIP UEs for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PsMeasFreqPriSwitch: Indicates whether frequency priorities specified by the PsPriority parameter take effect for coverage-based handovers of UEs running data services. If this option is selected, the eNodeB includes only intra-RAT neighboring frequencies with the highest priority based on the settings of the PsPriority parameter when the eNodeB delivers measurement configurations to UEs running data services for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not consider the settings of the PsPriority parameter when delivering measurement configurations to UEs running data services for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: FixedMeasObjIDSwitch(FixedMeasObjIDSwitch), VoipMeasFreqPriSwitch(VoipMeasFreqPriSwitch), PsMeasFreqPriSwitch(PsMeasFreqPriSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: FixedMeasObjIDSwitch, VoipMeasFreqPriSwitch, PsMeasFreqPriSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: FixedMeasObjIDSwitch:Off, VoipMeasFreqPriSwitch:Off, PsMeasFreqPriSwitch:On

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: FixedMeasObjIDSwitch: If this option is selected, UEs that preferentially measure neighboring frequencies with small MeasObjectIDs are more likely to measure neighboring frequencies with high priorities, and therefore these UEs can be preferentially handed over to cells on these neighboring frequencies.

VoipMeasFreqPriSwitch: If this option is selected, the frequencies measured by VoIP UEs for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers are fixed, and these UEs can be preferentially handed over to high-priority frequencies. This mechanism applies when operators require UEs to be handed over to low frequencies with good coverage, so that the number of handovers can be reduced during continuous UE movement and the voice quality can be improved. If the bandwidth of a low frequency is small, data service rates of VoIP UEs decrease.

PsMeasFreqPriSwitch: If this option is selected, the frequencies measured by UEs running data services for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers are fixed, and these UEs can be preferentially handed over to high-priority frequencies. This mechanism applies when operators require UEs to be handed over to low frequencies with good coverage, so that the number of handovers can be reduced during continuous UE movement. If this option is deselected, the measurement target frequency cannot be specified for coverage-based inter-frequency handovers of UEs running data services. In this case, multiple handovers may occur during UE movement.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
HarqAlgoSwitch Harq Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable HARQ-related algorithms.

TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch: Indicates whether to optimize the HARQ-ACK feedback mode for LTE TDD. If this option is selected, the HARQ-ACK feedback mode is reconfigured for a UE based on service conditions of the UE. If this option is deselected, the HARQ-ACK feedback mode is not reconfigured for a UE based on service conditions of the UE. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch(TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If the TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch is on, the TDD HARQ-ACK feedback mode is adaptively configured based on the PDCCH load and UE services in the cell, which leads to increased UEs with the TDD HARQ-ACK feedback mode configured to multiplexing mode. Compared with the bundling mode, the multiplexing mode increases the MCSs. However, due to no DTX state in multiplexing mode, the CCE aggregation level of PDCCH data cannot be accurately determined and the PDCCH power control cannot be accurately performed, which reduces the PDCCH reliability. As a result, the handover success rate, call drop rate, ping delay, and PUCCH coverage performance may deteriorate, while the cell throughput, single-user throughput, and user-perceived rate may increase. If the TddAckFbModeCfgOptSwitch is off, the TDD HARQ-ACK feedback mode is not reconfigured based on the PDCCH load and UE services. Most UEs use the bundling mode for HARQ-ACK feedback, without reducing the PDCCH reliability. In addition, the handover success rate, call drop rate, ping delay, PUCCH coverage performance, and access delay may be optimized, while the cell throughput, single-user throughput, and user-perceived rate may decrease.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
CovBasedInterFreqHoMode Coverage Based Inter Frequency Handover Mode

Meaning: Indicates the coverage-based inter-frequency handover mode.

If this parameter is set to HANDOVERIMMEDIATELY(Handover Immediately), the eNodeB immediately determines whether to perform a coverage-based inter-frequency handover when receiving a measurement report.

If this parameter is set to BASEDONSIGNALSTRENGTH(Based on Signal Strength), the eNodeB waits a moment and then determines whether to perform a coverage-based inter-frequency handover to the strongest neighboring cell indicated by the received measurement reports.

If this parameter is set to BASEDONFREQPRIORITY(Based on Frequency Priority), the eNodeB waits a moment and then determines whether to perform a coverage-based inter-frequency handover to a cell whose frequency has a relatively low priority.

This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: HANDOVERIMMEDIATELY(HANDOVERIMMEDIATELY), BASEDONSIGNALSTRENGTH(BASEDONSIGNALSTRENGTH), BASEDONFREQPRIORITY(BASEDONFREQPRIORITY)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: HANDOVERIMMEDIATELY, BASEDONSIGNALSTRENGTH, BASEDONFREQPRIORITY

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: None

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to HANDOVERIMMEDIATELY, the eNodeB immediately determines whether to perform a coverage-based inter-frequency handover when receiving a measurement report. This ensures that UEs are handed over in time. If this parameter is set to BASEDONSIGNALSTRENGTH, UEs can be handed over to the strongest neighboring cells. However, the waiting time puts off the handover, increases the handover failure rate, and causes service drops. If this parameter is set to BASEDONFREQPRIORITY, the possibility that UEs are handed over to neighboring cells whose frequency has a high priority increases. This helps these neighboring cells absorb more traffic, but puts off the handover, increases the handover failure rate, and causes service drops.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
LteUtcBroadcastSwitch Lte Utc Broadcast Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether the eNodeB broadcasts the coordinated universal time (UTC) in the LTE system over the air interface. If this parameter is set to 1, the eNodeB broadcasts the UTC in the LTE system using system information block type 16 (SIB16). If this parameter is set to 0, the eNodeB does not broadcast the UTC in the LTE system.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: Setting this parameter to ON increases air interface overheads for broadcasting UTC in the LTE system through SIB16.

Applicable Mode: L
CellSchStrategySwitch Cell Schedule Strategy Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable a specific scheduling policy in the uplink and downlink.

AbnUeSchSwitch: Indicates whether to disable scheduling of abnormal UEs. If this switch is on, abnormal UEs are not scheduled. If this switch is off, abnormal UEs are scheduled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: AbnUeSchSwitch(AbnUeSchSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: AbnUeSchSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: AbnUeSchSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: AbnUeSchSwitch: Turning off this switch has no impact on network performance. If this switch is on, scheduling of abnormal UEs is disabled in the uplink and downlink, and resource efficiency increases. As a result, cell throughput increases in scenarios where resources are insufficient. However, the scheduling delay increases, perceived throughput decreases, and service drop rate increases for UEs in weak coverage areas.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
SfnUplinkCompSwitch SFN Uplink Comp Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable uplink coordinated multipoint reception (UL CoMP) for an SFN cell.

IntraBaseBandSwitch: Indicates whether to enable intra-BBP UL CoMP for an SFN cell. Intra-BBP UL CoMP takes effect in an SFN cell only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterBaseBandSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-BBP UL CoMP for an SFN cell. Inter-BBP UL CoMP takes effect in an SFN cell only if this option is selected. The setting of this option is valid only if the IntraBaseBandSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ComCoverUlCompSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL CoMP when multiple RRUs provide the same coverage in an SFN cell. UL CoMP is enabled for the SFN cell in which multiple RRUs provide the same coverage only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch: Indicates whether to enable three-cell UL CoMP for an SFN cell. Three-cell UL CoMP is enabled for the SFN cell only if this option is selected. The setting of this option is valid only if the IntraBaseBandSwitch or InterBaseBandSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: IntraBaseBandSwitch(IntraBaseBandSwitch), InterBaseBandSwitch(InterBaseBandSwitch), ComCoverUlCompSwitch(ComCoverUlCompSwitch), SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch(SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: IntraBaseBandSwitch, InterBaseBandSwitch, ComCoverUlCompSwitch, SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: IntraBaseBandSwitch: Turning on this switch is recommended because intra-BBP UL CoMP can increase the average uplink throughput and improve the uplink coverage of an SFN cell.

InterBaseBandSwitch: Turning on this switch is recommended because inter-BBP UL CoMP can increase the average uplink throughput and improve the uplink coverage of an SFN cell.

ComCoverUlCompSwitch: Turning off this switch is recommended if multiple RF modules provide different coverages in an SFN cell.

SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch: Turning on this switch is recommended when an SFN cell is composed of three or more physical cells.

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • If the InterBaseBandSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the IntraBaseBandSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter must be selected.
  • If the SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the IntraBaseBandSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter must be selected.
  • The UlHighOrderCompfor2R option of the UlHighOrderCompSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the UlJointReception3CellSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter or the SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: IntraBaseBandSwitch: Turning on this switch increases the uplink throughput of cell edge users (CEUs in the overlapping coverage areas of multiple RF modules in an SFN cell) and the average uplink throughput of the SFN cell. The increase in the uplink CEU throughput indicates that the uplink coverage improves.

InterBaseBandSwitch: Turning on this switch enables inter-BBP UL CoMP in an SFN cell. This further increases the uplink CEU throughput and the average uplink throughput of the SFN cell. The increase in the uplink CEU throughput indicates that the uplink coverage improves.

ComCoverUlCompSwitch: Turning on this switch increases the uplink CEU throughput and the average uplink throughput of the SFN cell when multiple RF modules provide the same coverage in an SFN cell. The increase in the uplink CEU throughput indicates that the uplink coverage improves.

SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch: Turning on this switch enables UL CoMP to be performed on qualified UEs in three physical cells of an SFN cell. This further increases the uplink CEU throughput and the average uplink throughput of the SFN cell.

Applicable Mode: L
LowSpeedInterFreqHoSwitch Low Speed User Inter-frequency Handover Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the inter-frequency directional handover algorithm for low-speed UEs. If this switch is turned on, the eNodeB instructs low-speed UEs in RRC_CONNECTED mode on high-speed railway networks to be handed over to low-speed inter-frequency neighboring cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this switch is turned on, low-speed UEs are handed over to low-speed inter-frequency neighboring cells, ensuring that the capacity of high-speed railway networks is not occupied by UEs on public networks.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
RelaySwitch Relay Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable relay-related functions.

OutOfBandRelaySwitch: Indicates whether to enable out-of-band relay. If this option is selected, out-of-band relay is enabled. If this option is deselected, out-of-band relay is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InBandRelayDeNbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable in-band relay for the DeNB. If this option is selected, in-band relay is enabled for the DeNB. If this option is deselected, in-band relay is disabled for the DeNB. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InBandRelayReNbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable in-band relay for the ReNB. If this option is selected, in-band relay is enabled for the ReNB. If this option is deselected, in-band relay is disabled for the ReNB. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InBandRelayDistantDeploySw: Indicates whether to allow the first-hop ReNB to be deployed as far as 20 km from the DeNB in in-band relay scenarios. If this option is selected, the first-hop ReNB can be deployed as far as 20 km from the DeNB in in-band relay scenarios. If this option is deselected, the first-hop ReNB cannot be deployed as far as 20 km from the DeNB in in-band relay scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OutOfBandRelaySwitch(OutOfBandRelaySwitch), InBandRelayDeNbSwitch(InBandRelayDeNbSwitch), InBandRelayReNbSwitch(InBandRelayReNbSwitch), InBandRelayDistantDeploySw(InBandRelayDistantDeploySw)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, InBandRelayReNbSwitch, InBandRelayDistantDeploySw

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OutOfBandRelaySwitch:Off, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch:Off, InBandRelayReNbSwitch:Off, InBandRelayDistantDeploySw:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to STATIC or DYNAMIC when OutOfBandRelaySwitch under the RelaySwitch parameter is turned on.
  • The following conditions are mutually exclusive with each other: The EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to STATIC or DYNAMIC; the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter is selected.
  • The following conditions are mutually exclusive with each other: The EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to STATIC or DYNAMIC; the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter is selected.
  • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected when MBSFNShutDownSwitch under the DlSchSwitch parameter is turned on.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the MBSFNShutDownSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the MBSFNShutDownSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the TtiBundlingSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in this MO cannot be both selected.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the TtiBundlingSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the TtiBundlingSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayDistantDeploySw option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: In the RelaySwitch parameter under the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option is selected and the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option is deselected.
  • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch and InBandRelayDeNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both selected.
  • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch and InBandRelayReNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both selected.
  • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch and InBandRelayReNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both selected.
  • The FeicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF if the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The FeicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF if the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected if the IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, or InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • In the same CellAlgoSwitch MO, the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter and the TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the UlInterSiteIcSwitch option of the UplinkIcSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the UlInterSiteIcSwitch option of the UplinkIcSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch (an option of the RelaySwitch parameter) and TurboReceiverSwitch cannot be both on.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, InBandRelayReNbSwitch (an option of the RelaySwitch parameter) and TurboReceiverSwitch cannot be both on.
  • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the MumimoPuschPsicSwitch option of the PsicSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the MumimoPuschPsicSwitch option of the PsicSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.
  • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the same MO cannot be both selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: OutOfBandRelaySwitch: If this option is selected, the number of common UEs that can access the cell decreases, both the total throughput of common UEs in the cell and the peak throughput of a single common UE decrease (the decrease rate is in positive correlation with the number of resources occupied by relay subframes), and the relayed UEs can access the cell. If this option is deselected, the number of common UEs that can access the cell increases, the throughput of common UEs may increase, and relayed UEs that have accessed the cell are released, and services of the relayed UEs are interrupted.

InBandRelayDeNbSwitch: If this option is selected, the number of common UEs that can access the cell decreases, both the total throughput of common UEs in the cell and the peak throughput of a single common UE decrease (the decrease rate is in positive correlation with the number of resources occupied by backhaul subframes), and the backhaul UEs can access the cell. If this option is deselected, the number of common UEs that can access the cell increases, the throughput of common UEs may increase, backhaul UEs that have accessed the cell are released, and services of the relayed UEs are interrupted.

InBandRelayReNbSwitch: If this option is selected, UEs cannot be scheduled on certain subframes. As a result, the number of UEs that can access the cell decreases, both the total throughput of UEs in the cell and the peak throughput of a single UE decrease (the decrease rate is in negative correlation with the number of resources occupied by in-band relay backhaul subframes). If this option is deselected, the number of UEs that can access the cell increases and the UE throughput may increase if S1 and X2 interfaces are available. If S1 and X2 interfaces are unavailable, UEs cannot access the cell.

InBandRelayDistantDeploySw: When relay UEs are deployed from the DeNB at a distance greater than 9.5 km and less than 20 km, this option must be selected. This will reduce interference of relay UEs on ReNB symbols but have no impact on common UEs. If this option is deselected in such scenarios, relay UEs interfere with ReNB symbols. When relay UEs are deployed from the DeNB at a distance less than 9.5 km, deselect this option. If this option is selected in such scenarios, the uplink throughput of relay UEs served by the DeNB and peak throughput of a single relay UE decrease.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
InterFreqDirectHoSwitch Inter-frequency Directional Handover Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the inter-frequency directional handover algorithm. If this switch is turned on, the eNodeB instructs UEs in RRC_CONNECTED mode on high-speed railway networks to measure inter-frequency cells so that the UEs can be handed over to high-speed inter-frequency cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
PwrDeratSwitch Power Derating Switch

Meaning: Indicates the switch used to determine which power derating scheme to use. If this parameter is set to OFF, the shared channel transmit power is reduced but the RS power, PSCH power, and power of other common channels are not reduced, which helps ensure coverage but decreases cell throughput. With this parameter setting, the configured RS power cannot exceed half of the maximum transmit power of the cell. For details about how to calculate the RS power and the maximum transmit power of a cell, see 3GPP TS 36.213. If this parameter is set to ON, both of the shared channel transmit power and RS power are reduced, which causes coverage to shrink but helps to maintain cell throughput. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to OFF, the cell coverage does not shrink and cell throughput decreases. If this parameter is set to ON, the cell coverage shrinks and cell throughput does not decrease.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
DetectionAlgoSwitch DetectionAlgoSwitch

Meaning: CqiReliableSwitch: Indicates whether channel quality indicator (CQI) detection errors can be identified when interference occurs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB can identify CQI detection errors when interference occurs and use a historical CQI. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB cannot identify CQI detection errors when interference occurs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch: Indicates whether to collect statistics about CQI values detected within the time specified by DrxInactivityTimer triggered by false SR detections. The eNodeB does not collect such statistics only when this option is selected.

GUI Value Range: CqiReliableSwitch(CqiReliableSwitch), DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch(DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: CqiReliableSwitch, DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: CqiReliableSwitch:Off

DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch:On

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: CQI-related performance indicators improve when the DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch option is selected.

Applicable Mode: L
PucchIRCEnhance Pucch IRC Enhance

Meaning: Indicates whether the eNodeB determines interference strength. Determining interference strength ensures accurate adaptive switching between maximum ratio combining (MRC) and interference rejection combining (IRC). This parameter takes effect only when PucchIrcSwitch of the IrcSwitch parameter is turned on.

Turning on both this switch and PucchIrcSwitch at the same time provides better PUCCH detection performance than turning off both switches.

If PucchIrcSwitch is turned on, turning on this switch decreases the number of false detections and also slightly decreases PUCCH detection performance. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: ON(On)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: The detection performance is better when the PucchIrcSwitch option of the IrcSwitch parameter is selected and the PucchIrcEnhance parameter is set to ON than when the PucchIrcSwitch option is deselected.

If the PucchIrcSwitch option of the IrcSwitch parameter is selected, the probability of false detection and the uplink BLER decrease when the setting of the PucchIrcEnhance parameter is changed from OFF to ON; however, the detection performance slightly deteriorates and the ping delay slightly increases.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
AcBarAlgoforDynSwitch Ac Bar Algorithm Switch for Dynamic

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable dynamic access barring algorithms. This parameter controls whether to enable dynamic access barring on mobile-originated (MO) signaling, MO data, service-specific access control (SSAC) for multimedia telephony (MMTEL) voice services, or SSAC for MMTEL video services. This parameter takes effect only when the AcBarAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC.

DYNAMIC_FOR_MO: Indicates whether to enable dynamic access barring on UEs running MO signaling or MO data.

If this switch is on, dynamic access barring applies to UEs running MO signaling or MO data.

If this switch is off, dynamic access barring does not apply to UEs running MO signaling or MO data. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC: Indicates whether to enable dynamic access barring on UEs running MMTEL voice services or MMTEL video services.

If this switch is on, dynamic access barring applies to UEs running MMTEL voice services or MMTEL video services.

If this switch is off, dynamic access barring does not apply to UEs running MMTEL voice services or MMTEL video services. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: DYNAMIC_FOR_MO(DYNAMIC_FOR_MO), DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC(DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: DYNAMIC_FOR_MO, DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: DYNAMIC_FOR_MO:On, DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if AcBarAlgoSwitch is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC, either the DYNAMIC_FOR_MO or DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC option of AcBarAlgoforDynSwitch must be selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: DYNAMIC_FOR_MO: If this switch is on, the access probability factors for MO Data and MO signaling are dynamically adjusted based on the current cell load. In this way, the probability of UEs accessing the cell can be controlled to relieve the cell load. However, user experience of barred UEs is affected. If this switch is off, it has the opposite effects.

DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC: If this switch is on, the access probability factors for MMTEL voice services and MMTEL video services are dynamically adjusted based on the current cell load. In this way, the probability of UEs accessing the cell can be controlled to relieve the cell load. However, user experience of barred UEs is affected. If this switch is off, it has the opposite effects.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
CreSwitch CRE Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether the CellRangeExpansion parameter takes effect in a HetNet.

If this parameter is set to OFF and eICIC is enabled, the CellRangeExpansion parameter takes effect. If this parameter is set to OFF and eICIC is disabled, the CellRangeExpansion parameter does not take effect. If this parameter is set to ON, the CellRangeExpansion parameter takes effect for all UEs (including R8 and R9 UEs) in the cell. For macro and micro cells in a HetNet, it is recommended that this parameter be set to the same value. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON for a HetNet, the CellRangeExpansion parameter can be set to increase the number of UEs that a micro cell can accommodate, balance loads between macro and micro cells, and improve cell edge coverage. However, if the CellRangeExpansion parameter value exceeds a threshold while eICIC is not enabled, the handover success rate may decrease and the service drop rate may increase. If this parameter is set to OFF, a micro cell can accommodate a few number of UEs; in this case, the CellRangeExpansion parameter cannot be used to balance loads between macro and micro cells or improve cell edge coverage.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
BackoffAlgoSwitch Backoff Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable MLB for low-efficiency UEs.

InterFreqBackoffSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-neighboring-cell inter-frequency MLB for low-efficiency UEs. If this option is selected, inter-frequency MLB is enabled for low-efficiency UEs. If this option is deselected, inter-frequency MLB is disabled for low-efficiency UEs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: InterFreqBackoffSwitch(InterFreqBackoffSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: InterFreqBackoffSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: InterFreqBackoffSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: When the InterFreqBackoffSwitch check box is selected, low-efficiency UEs are selected for MLB, thereby improving the average cell throughput. When this check box is deselected, low-efficiency UEs are not selected for MLB. In this situation, the average cell throughput cannot reach its maximum.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
HoAllowedSwitch Handover Allowed Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable eNodeB-level handovers to be activated in the cell when related eNodeB-level handover switches are on.

SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw: Indicates whether to enable service-based inter-frequency handover to be activated in the cell. Service-based inter-frequency handover is allowed to be activated in the cell only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch: Indicates whether to differentiate mobility policies for GERAN operators. When this option is selected, different network policies can be applied to different GERAN operators. Such policies include SRVCC and SI obtaining through RIM procedures. When this option is deselected, different network policies cannot be applied to different GERAN operators. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranCsfbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CSFB to UTRAN. When this option is selected, CSFB to UTRAN is allowed. When this option is deselected, the UTRAN CSFB algorithm is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranCsfbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CSFB to GERAN. When this option is selected, CSFB to GERAN is allowed. When this option is deselected, the GERAN CSFB algorithm is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranFlashCsfbSwitch: This option does not take effect if UtranCsfbSwitch is off. When UtranFlashCsfbSwitch is on, flash CSFB to UTRAN is enabled, and UTRAN system information is carried during redirection. When UtranFlashCsfbSwitch is off, flash CSFB to UTRAN is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranFlashCsfbSwitch: This option does not take effect if GeranCsfbSwitch is off. When GeranFlashCsfbSwitch is on, flash CSFB to GERAN is enabled, and GERAN system information is carried during redirection. When GeranFlashCsfbSwitch is off, flash CSFB to GERAN is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch: This option does not take effect if BlindHoSwitch is off. If BlindHoSwitch is on and UFCsfbBlindHoDisSwitch is also on, CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch does not take effect and UEs fall back to UTRAN or GERAN through ultra-flash CSFB. When this option is selected, CSFB adaptive blind handover is enabled. In this situation, an optimal handover mode is used depending on UE position in the CSFB scenario. When this option is deselected, CSFB adaptive blind handover is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranCsfbSteeringSwitch: UTRAN CSFB steering is enabled when this option is selected. In this case, RRC_IDLE UEs can use separate configuration policies when triggering CSFB. UTRAN CSFB steering is disabled when this option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranCsfbSteeringSwitch: GERAN CSFB steering is enabled when this option is selected. In this case, RRC_IDLE UEs can use separate configuration policies when triggering CSFB. GERAN CSFB steering is disabled when this option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ServBasedHoBackSwitch: Indicates whether to allow service- or service-request-based inter-frequency handovers to source frequencies for UEs that have been handed over from the source frequencies to target frequencies. Such handovers to the source frequencies are allowed only if this option is selected. This switch takes effect for TDD cells with ServiceHoBearerPolicy set to SERVICE_HO_QCI_ALLOWED and does not take effect for TDD cells with ServiceHoBearerPolicy set to SERVICE_HO_QCI_NOT_ALLOWED. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch: If this option is selected, service-request-based inter-frequency handovers are enabled and UEs using a specific type of service can be handed over to inter-frequency cells. If this option is deselected, service-request-based inter-frequency handovers are disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch: If this option is selected and the preferred CSFB policy is redirection, during CSFB of RRC_IDLE UEs, the eNodeB sends redirection messages without activating the security mode and performing RRC connection reconfiguration. However, if the eNodeB needs to query the UE capability information during redirection-based CSFB for RRC_IDLE UEs and the UeCapbEnquirySecurityEnhSwitch option of the ProtocolMsgOptSwitch parameter in the GlobalProcSwitch MO is selected, then the security mode activation, UE capability transfer, and RRC connection release procedures are processed in serial mode. In accordance with the security requirements of 3GPP CR (R2-1714284), this option takes effect only for redirections to UTRAN cells. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ServBasedNrHoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable service-based handover from E-UTRAN to NR. Service-based handover from E-UTRAN to NR is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw(SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw), GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch(GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch), UtranCsfbSwitch(UtranCsfbSwitch), GeranCsfbSwitch(GeranCsfbSwitch), UtranFlashCsfbSwitch(UtranFlashCsfbSwitch), GeranFlashCsfbSwitch(GeranFlashCsfbSwitch), CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch(CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch), UtranCsfbSteeringSwitch(UtranCsfbSteeringSwitch), GeranCsfbSteeringSwitch(GeranCsfbSteeringSwitch), ServBasedHoBackSwitch(ServBasedHoBackSwitch), ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch(ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch), IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch(IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch), ServBasedNrHoSwitch(ServBasedNrHoSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw, GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch, UtranCsfbSwitch, GeranCsfbSwitch, UtranFlashCsfbSwitch, GeranFlashCsfbSwitch, CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch, UtranCsfbSteeringSwitch, GeranCsfbSteeringSwitch, ServBasedHoBackSwitch, ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch, IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch, ServBasedNrHoSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw:On, GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch:Off, UtranCsfbSwitch:Off, GeranCsfbSwitch:Off, UtranFlashCsfbSwitch:Off, GeranFlashCsfbSwitch:Off, CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch:Off, UtranCsfbSteeringSwitch:Off, GeranCsfbSteeringSwitch:Off, ServBasedHoBackSwitch:Off, ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch:Off, IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch:Off, ServBasedNrHoSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw: If ServiceBasedInterFreqHoSwitch of the HoAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is off, the setting of SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw has no impact on network performance. If ServiceBasedInterFreqHoSwitch of the HoAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is on, turning on SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw enables the cell to use the same service steering policies as other cells and improves the network performance based on service steering; turning off SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw disables the cell to use service steering.

GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch: When this option is selected, different network policies can be applied to different GERAN operators. Such policies include SRVCC and SI obtaining through RIM procedures. In this way, operators supporting SRVCC or RIM procedures can select an optimal network policy to achieve the best performance. When this option is deselected, a uniform network policy is applied to different GERAN operators. That is, operators cannot utilize the best network performance according to their network characteristics.

ServBasedHoBackSwitch: When this option is deselected, service steering cannot be implemented. When this option is selected, service steering can be implemented, improving network performance.

ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch: If this option is selected, the E-RAB setup success rate of voice-related QCIs decreases and the number of voice service handovers increases.

IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the number of signaling exchanges for CSFB decreases, shortening the CSFB delay.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
NCellClassMgtSw Neighbor Cell Classification Mgt Switch

Meaning: Indicates neighboring cell classification management. This parameter includes the following options:

INTRA_RAT_NCELL_CLASS_SW: If this option is selected, intra-RAT event-triggered ANR or intra-RAT fast ANR is enabled, and the ANR indicator is set to ALLOWED for this frequency, the eNodeB automatically marks the NCellClassLabel parameter value as FORMAL or EXTENDED for intra-RAT neighboring cells based on handover-related statistic results. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NCELL_CLASS_HO_POLICY_SW: If this option is selected, intra-RAT event-triggered ANR is enabled, and the ANR indicator is set to ALLOWED for this frequency, target cells for handovers must be determined based on E-UTRAN cell global identifier (ECGI) reading on intra-RAT neighboring cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
