Recommended Value: None

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
DMIMOAlgoSwitch D-MIMO Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable D-MIMO algorithms. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

DmimoJTSwitch: Indicates whether to enable D-MIMO joint transmission. If this option is selected, D-MIMO joint transmission is enabled. If this option is deselected, D-MIMO joint transmission is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable scheduling attribute optimization for D-MIMO UEs. If this option is selected, scheduling attribute optimization is enabled for D-MIMO UEs. If this option is deselected, scheduling attribute optimization is disabled for D-MIMO UEs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

InterCellDmimoJTSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-cell D-MIMO. If this option is selected, inter-cell D-MIMO is enabled. If this option is deselected, inter-cell D-MIMO is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: DmimoJTSwitch(DmimoJTSwitch), DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch(DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch), InterCellDmimoJTSwitch(InterCellDmimoJTSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: DmimoJTSwitch, DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch, InterCellDmimoJTSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: DmimoJTSwitch:Off, DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch:Off, InterCellDmimoJTSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • If DMIMOJTSwitch of DMIMOAlgoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, MuBfSwitch of MuBfAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • The MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected if the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both selected.
  • The BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected if the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • The DmimoJTSwitch and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be selected at the same time.
  • The DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: DmimoJTSwitch: If this option is selected, average downlink user experience in multi-RRU cells and the experience of CEUs in such cells improve, but the RAR success rate decreases and the access delay increases. In this case, the total number of CCEs used in the uplink and downlink per TTI increases if CCE resources are sufficient, and CCE allocation failures per TTI increase if CCE resources are insufficient. If this option is deselected, average downlink user experience of multi-RRU cells and the experience of CEUs in such cells do not improve.

InterCellDmimoJTSwitch: If this option is selected, average downlink user experience and the experience of CEUs in cells improve, but the RAR success rate decreases and the access delay increases. In this case, the total number of CCEs used in the uplink and downlink per TTI increases if CCE resources are sufficient, and CCE allocation failures per TTI increase if CCE resources are insufficient. If this option is deselected, average downlink user experience and the experience of CEUs in cells do not improve.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
UlAmrcMode Uplink Rate Control Mode

Meaning: Indicates the uplink voice rate control mode.

If this parameter is set to ULRATECTRL_OFF, uplink voice rate control does not take effect. If RTP packets are encrypted, this parameter must be set to ULRATECTRL_OFF.

If this parameter is set to ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL, the eNodeB executes uplink voice AMR control.

If this parameter is set to ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL, the session border controller (SBC) on the EPC executes uplink voice AMR control. The SBC must be a Huawei SBC supporting voice AMR control.

If this parameter is set to ULEVSC_ENB_CONTROL, the eNodeB executes uplink EVS rate control.

If this parameter is set to ADAPTIVE_ENB_CONTROL, the eNodeB executes uplink AMR/EVS adaptive coding rate control. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DisuseStatement: The enumerated value ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL in this parameter will be deleted in later versions. Although the configuration interface in this version supports synchronization and delivery of the setting of this enumerated value, the system does not use this enumerated value. Therefore, avoid using this enumerated value ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL.

GUI Value Range: ULRATECTRL_OFF(ULRATECTRL_OFF), ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL(ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL), ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL(ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL), ULEVSC_ENB_CONTROL(ULEVSC_ENB_CONTROL), ADAPTIVE_ENB_CONTROL(ADAPTIVE_ENB_CONTROL)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: ULRATECTRL_OFF, ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL, ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL, ULEVSC_ENB_CONTROL, ADAPTIVE_ENB_CONTROL

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: ULRATECTRL_OFF(ULRATECTRL_OFF)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If uplink voice rate control is enabled and the uplink channel quality is good, a high voice coding rate is used. If the uplink channel quality is poor, a low coding rate is used to improve uplink voice quality.

If the percentage of times the initial voice coding rate is increased to a higher voice coding rate is large, more CCE and RB resources are consumed by voice services. In CCE-limited or full-buffer service scenarios, the traffic volume and throughput of data services are more likely to decrease. If the percentage of times the initial voice coding rate is decreased to a lower voice coding rate is large, less CCE and RB resources are consumed by voice services. In CCE-limited or full-buffer service scenarios, the traffic volume and throughput of data services are more likely to increase.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
AmrcAlgoSwitch Rate Control Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates the switches of algorithm policies used for adjusting the uplink speech rate and related functions.

UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw: Indicates whether the adjusted uplink AMR voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate for this call. The adjusted uplink AMR voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlAmrCheckSw: Indicates whether to adjust the uplink AMR voice coding rate if the eNodeB has not obtained the rate set information about this session and the UlAmrcMode parameter is set to ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL. The uplink AMR voice coding rate can be adjusted only if this option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoiceCodingModeMeasSw: Indicates whether to measure the distribution of the numbers of uplink and downlink voice packets in different voice coding schemes on a per cell basis. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not measure the distribution of the numbers of uplink and downlink voice packets in different voice coding schemes on a per cell basis. If this option is selected, the corresponding counter is incremented based on the frame types (voice frame and silence frame in different voice coding schemes) each time the eNodeB PDCP layer receives an uplink or downlink PDCP SDU with a QCI of 1 in a cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoLTELoadAmrcSw: Indicates whether to enable voice-load-based AMRC/EVS rate control. If voice-load-based AMRC/EVS rate control is enabled, the AMRC/EVS rate is reduced when voice load of the cell is high. Voice-load-based AMRC/EVS rate control is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlEvsExceedingInitialSw: Indicates whether the adjusted uplink EVS voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate for this call. The adjusted uplink EVS voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw: Indicates whether to enable AMRC for uplink MCPTT voice services. AMRC is enabled for uplink MCPTT voice services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw(UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw), UlAmrCheckSw(UlAmrCheckSw), VoiceCodingModeMeasSw(VoiceCodingModeMeasSw), VoLTELoadAmrcSw(VoLTELoadAmrcSw), UlEvsExceedingInitialSw(UlEvsExceedingInitialSw), UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw(UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw, UlAmrCheckSw, VoiceCodingModeMeasSw, VoLTELoadAmrcSw, UlEvsExceedingInitialSw, UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw:Off, UlAmrCheckSw:On, VoiceCodingModeMeasSw:Off, VoLTELoadAmrcSw:Off, UlEvsExceedingInitialSw:Off, UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw: If this option is selected and the uplink AMR voice coding rate increases significantly, the uplink voice quality is improved. However, the voice packet loss rate will slightly increase and the CCE and RB resources consumed by voice services will increase, causing a slight decrease in UE- and cell-specific throughput.

UlAmrCheckSw: If the UlAmrcMode parameter is set to ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL, this option is deselected, and the adjusted target coding rate is not adopted by this session, then one-way audio may occur if the coding rate is incompatible on the UE.

VoiceCodingModeMeasSw: If this option is selected and a large number of UEs are processing voice services, the CPU usage of the BBP (indicated by L.Traffic.Board.UPlane.CPULoad.AVG) will increase. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

VoLTELoadAmrcSw: If this option is selected, the uplink air interface load of the cell is high, and uplink VoLTE service packets are fragmented, then the AMR for voice services will be decreased to reduce uplink PRB and CCE congestion in the cell. As a result, the voice service quality may deteriorate. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

UlEvsExceedingInitialSw: If this option is selected and the percentage of times the EVS coding rate is increased to a rate higher than the initial coding rate is large, the uplink voice quality is improved. However, the voice packet loss rate slightly increases and more CCE and RB resources are consumed by voice services, causing a slight decrease in UE- and cell-specific throughput.

UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw: If this option is selected, a high voice coding rate is used when the uplink channel quality is good and a low voice coding rate is used when the uplink channel quality is poor, which improves uplink voice quality. If the percentage of times the voice coding rate is increased to a rate higher than the initial voice coding rate is large, more CCE and RB resources are consumed by voice services. In CCE-limited or full-buffer service scenarios, the traffic volume and throughput of data services are more likely to decrease. If the percentage of times the voice coding rate is decreased to a rate lower than the initial voice coding rate is large, fewer CCE and RB resources are consumed by voice services. In CCE-limited or full-buffer service scenarios, the traffic volume and throughput of data services are more likely to increase. If this option is deselected, there is no impact on radio network performance.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
CrsIcSwitch CRS Interference Cancellation Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to deliver information about intra-frequency neighboring cells for UEs to cancel CRS interference. CRS-IC is independent from FeICIC. CRS interference can be canceled on a per subframe basis.

If this parameter is set to ON, CRS-IC is enabled and the eNodeB delivers information about intra-frequency neighboring cells for UEs to cancel CRS interference.

If this parameter is set to OFF, CRS-IC is disabled and the eNodeB does not deliver information about intra-frequency neighboring cells for UEs to cancel CRS interference. Therefore, UEs cannot cancel CRS interference. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON, CRS interference decreases between densely deployed cells and cell-edge UEs have higher throughput. If this parameter is set to OFF, the CRS interference between densely deployed cells causes lower throughput for cell-edge UEs.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
EnhChnCalSwitch Enhanced Channel Calibration Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable enhanced channel calibration.

QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable fast channel calibration. If this option is selected, fast channel calibration is enabled. If this option is deselected, fast channel calibration is disabled. This option takes effect only when UBBP boards are used and does not take effect in the case of LBBP boards or in massive MIMO scenarios. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable channel calibration for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs. If this option is selected, channel calibration takes effect for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs. If this option is deselected, channel calibration does not take effect for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH(QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH), TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH(TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH, TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH:Off

TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH: If this option is enabled, the calibration period is short and satisfactory channel calibration can be achieved, improving the downlink beamforming performance. However, more air interface and processing resources are used. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH: If this option is selected, channel calibration takes effect for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs, increasing the downlink throughput of such ports. If this option is deselected, the downlink throughput of such ports cannot be maximized.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
NbCellAlgoSwitch NB-IoT Cell Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates the NB-IoT cell-level algorithm switches.

IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH: Indicates whether eDRX paging is supported. eDRX paging can be delivered in the local cell only when this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the NB-IoT cell coverage extension function. When this option is selected, the NB-IoT cell coverage extension function is supported, and the eNodeB supports coverage extension through scheduling. When this option is deselected, common coverage is supported, which means that coverage extension cannot be achieved. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

MULTITONE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the NB-IoT Multi-tone feature. For uplink data transmission, when this option is selected, an eNodeB can allocate multiple subcarriers at a time to a multi-tone-capable UE. When this option is deselected, the Multi-tone feature is not supported, and the eNodeB allocates only one subcarrier at a time to a UE for uplink data transmission. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

SI_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_CFG_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the adaptive configuration of the NB-IoT system information (SI) frame offset. If this option is selected, adaptive configuration takes effect for NB-IoT SI frame offset. If this option is deselected, adaptive configuration does not take effect for NB-IoT SI frame offset, and the NB-IoT SI frame offset is fixed and cannot be adaptively configured. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH: Indicates whether the eNodeB supports dual-HARQ-process scheduling. If this option is selected, the eNodeB supports dual-HARQ-process scheduling and performs dual-HARQ-process scheduling for dual-HARQ-capable UEs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB supports only single-HARQ-process scheduling. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable adaptive step adjustment in the NB-IoT cell. If this option is selected, the eNodeB uses a large step to adjust AMC to accelerate IBLER convergence when it determines that large-step adjustment is required based on HARQ feedback from the UE. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB uses a fixed step to adjust AMC. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH: Indicates whether the eNodeB needs to obtain UE information from the UE and MME in control plane CIoT EPS optimization. In control plane CIoT EPS optimization, the eNodeB needs to obtain UE capability information from the UE and requests the UE information from the MME through the Retrieve UE Information message based on the S-TMSI contained in Msg3 only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH: Indicates whether to allow for scheduling using an extended TBS. If this option is selected, the eNodeB can use the extended TBS to schedule NB2 UEs that comply with 3GPP Release 14. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB schedules UEs using non-extended TBSs as specified in 3GPP Release 13. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable downlink scheduling optimization. NB-IoT cells support downlink signaling MCS index decrease and downlink retransmission mechanism optimization only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the RU adaptation algorithm for uplink resource scheduling. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the UCI repetition count adaptation algorithm to adjust the number of ACK or NACK repetitions. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable service-based QoS management. If this option is selected, NB-IoT UEs allocated QCIs can be scheduled based on QoS in the cell. If this option is deselected, NB-IoT UEs cannot be scheduled based on QoS in the cell. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW: Indicates whether to allow UEs to initiate RRC connection reestablishment in control plane CIoT EPS optimization mode in the cell. UEs are allowed to initiate RRC connection reestablishment in control plane CIoT EPS optimization mode only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH: Indicates whether to support high-speed mobility. If this option is selected, the eNodeB improves the performance of high-speed UEs by mitigating the Doppler effect. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform any differentiated processing for high-speed UEs. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the multi-carrier enhancement function for NB-IoT. If this option is selected, multi-carrier enhancement is supported, and paging and random access can be performed for UEs on non-anchor carriers. If this option is deselected, multi-carrier enhancement is not supported. In this case, paging and random access can be performed for UEs only on anchor carriers. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW: Indicates whether to adaptively adjust the maximum number of preamble transmissions. If this option is selected, the eNodeB adaptively adjusts the maximum number of preamble transmissions based on the network load. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not adaptively adjust the maximum number of preamble transmissions. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to allow power offset configuration for UEs of power class 6 at cell selection or reselection and whether to optimize the downlink scheduling of UEs of power class 6. The configuration is allowed and the scheduling is optimized only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable NPDCCH offset adaptation. If this option is selected, the NPDCCH offset of the UE-specific search space is adaptively configured. If this option is deselected, it is specified by the PdcchOffset parameter in the PrbPdcchCeConfig MO. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH: Indicates whether to use interference randomization for NPDSCH and NPDCCH. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH: Indicates whether to support cell coverage with a radius of 100 km. If this option is selected, cell coverage with a radius of 100 km is supported. If this option is deselected, cell coverage with a radius of 35 km is supported. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

GUI Value Range: IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH(IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH), COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH(COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH), MULTITONE_SWITCH(MULTITONE_SWITCH), SI_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_CFG_SWITCH(SI_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_CFG_SWITCH), DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH(DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH), DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH(DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH), ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH(ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH), RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH(RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH), TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH(TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH), RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH(RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH), UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH(UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH), SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH(SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH), CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW(CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW), HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH(HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH), MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH(MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH), NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH(NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH), DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH(DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH), ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW(ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW), PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW(PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW), 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH(100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH, COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH, MULTITONE_SWITCH, SI_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_CFG_SWITCH, DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH, DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH, ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH, RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH, TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH, RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH, UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH, SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH, CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW, HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH, MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH, NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH, DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH, ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW, PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW, 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH:Off, COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH:Off, MULTITONE_SWITCH:Off, SI_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_CFG_SWITCH:Off, DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH:Off, ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH:Off, RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH:Off, TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH:Off, DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH:Off, RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH:Off, UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH:Off, SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH:Off, CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW:Off, HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH:Off, MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH:Off, ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW:Off, PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW:Off, NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH:Off, DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH:Off, 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: N/A

Impact on Radio Network Performance: IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH: If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not deliver eDRX paging. If eDRX has been enabled on any UEs in the cell, paging failure occurs.

COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH: If this option is selected, UEs can normally perform services in the 20 dB coverage enhancement scenario. If this option is deselected, the service drop rate of UEs increases or UEs may not be able to perform services in poor coverage scenarios.

MULTITONE_SWITCH: If this option is selected, in areas with favorable coverage, the single-UE peak uplink data rate of a multi-tone-capable UE increases significantly, the transmission delay decreases accordingly, and the UE power consumption decreases. Deselecting this option leads to the opposite effects.

DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected in downlink co-channel interference scenarios, the downlink signaling or data demodulation success rate of NB-IoT UEs increases. If there is no downlink co-channel interference, more cell resources are consumed, decreasing the cell capacity.

DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH: When this option is selected, the eNodeB can use dual-HARQ-process scheduling for dual-HARQ-capable UEs. Dual-HARQ-process scheduling delivers higher peak uplink and downlink rates for these UEs than single-HARQ-process scheduling. When this option is deselected, the eNodeB can use only single-HARQ-process scheduling, the peak uplink and downlink rates of UEs are lower than when dual-HARQ-process scheduling is used.

ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH: When this option is selected, large steps can be used to adjust MCSs to accelerate IBLER convergence, therefore increasing downlink UE throughput. When this option is deselected, a fixed step is used and the IBLER convergence is slow for UEs requiring large-step adjustment, affecting downlink throughput of these UEs.

RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH: When this option is selected, the eNodeB sends a Retrieve UE Information message to the core network to obtain UE information in control plane CIoT EPS optimization, increasing the data transmission delay during access due to the related message exchange. If the MME does not support UE information retrieval, access-related counter values deteriorate because message responses time out over the S1 interface. When this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not send a Retrieve UE Information message to the core network to obtain UE information. As a result, the UE capability cannot be fully utilized due to the failure to obtain UE capability information.

TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH: When this option is selected, the eNodeB can use an extended TBS to schedule NB2 UEs that comply with 3GPP Release 14. As a result, the peak uplink and downlink rates of these UEs significantly increase and the transmission delay decreases. When this option is deselected, the eNodeB uses non-extended TBSs to schedule UEs as specified in 3GPP Release 13. As a result, the peak uplink and downlink rates of NB2 UEs significantly decrease, the transmission delay increases, and UE power consumption also increases.

RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: When this option is selected, the eNodeB can increase the single-user peak uplink throughput of UEs that support only 3GPP Release 13.

UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: When this option is selected, the eNodeB adjusts the number of UCI repetitions for UEs that are admitted in the cell center in accordance with ECL1 or ECL2 in interference-limited scenarios, reducing power consumption of these UEs and interference to neighboring cells. When this option is deselected, the number of UCI repetitions of cell-center UEs is large and the UE TX power is high, and therefore interference to neighboring cells is high.

SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the power consumption of UEs that have high requirements for power consumption is significantly reduced using the DRX function, and the coverage performance is improved for UEs under poor coverage.

CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW: If this option is selected, a moving UE can initiate RRC connection reestablishment in control plane CIoT EPS optimization mode in the cell and the source eNodeB can send the downlink data (that has not been transmitted to the UE) back to the MME, which then forwards the data to the target eNodeB. This improves data transmission continuity for the UE and reduces the number of data retransmissions. If this option is deselected, UEs cannot initiate RRC connection reestablishment in control plane CIoT EPS optimization mode in the cell. As a result, data transmission is interrupted for moving UEs and the number of data retransmissions increases.

HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB mitigates the uplink channel Doppler effect for high-speed UEs, improving network access performance and increasing uplink throughput in high-speed mobility scenarios. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not mitigate the uplink channel Doppler effect for high-speed UEs, causing the network access performance of these UEs to deteriorate and decreasing the uplink throughput of these UEs.

MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH: If this option is selected, non-anchor carriers support paging and random access, improving cell paging and access capacity. However, interference between carriers increases, the IBLER increases, and the cell throughput decreases.

ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW: If this option is selected, the maximum number of preamble transmissions is dynamically adjusted based on the network load. When the network load is high, the maximum number of preamble transmissions is changed to the minimum value among the GUI setting of the PreambleTransMax parameter, the MaxNumPreambleAttempt parameter setting for coverage level 0, and the MaxNumPreambleAttempt parameter setting for coverage level 1. The coverage level is not increased for UEs, which increases the access success rate. When the network load is low, the maximum number of preamble transmissions is restored to the value of the PreambleTransMax parameter in the RACHCfg MO.

PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW: If this option is selected, cell selection and reselection parameters and neighboring frequency parameters can be set for UEs of power class 6 to enable these UEs to camp on cells with better signal quality, and the number of downlink repetitions can be adjusted for UEs of power class 6 to reduce downlink resource wastage by these UEs.

NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the cell throughput increases, but the BER slightly increases in the access procedure. When multiple UEs (for example, more than five UEs) initiate access simultaneously, the RRC connection setup success rate decreases. If this option is deselected, the cell throughput decreases.

DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the interference resistance performance of NPDCCH and NPDSCH improves, increasing the downlink throughput of UEs.

100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the random access success rate decreases. If this option is deselected, the random access success rate increases.

Applicable Mode: L-NB-IOT
MultiCnConnFreqPriSw Multi Op Freq Priority For Connected Mode Sw

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the policy of frequency priority for the connected mode in handovers. If this parameter is set to ON, the frequency priority for the connected mode associated with the ConnFreqPriority parameter in the RatFreqPriorityGroup MO takes effect and the frequency priority policy in handovers is supported in RAN sharing scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch SRS-Free SCC BF Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable beamforming for UEs on SCCs without SRSs. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

NoSrsSccBfSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB allows a CA UE on an SCC without SRSs to use weight information of another SCell with SRSs of the UE to enter beamforming mode. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB disables beamforming for CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs.

NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch: If this option is selected, CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs can be paired for MU beamforming. If this option is deselected, CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs cannot be paired for MU beamforming.

GUI Value Range: NoSrsSccBfSwitch(NoSrsSccBfSwitch), NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch(NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: NoSrsSccBfSwitch, NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: NoSrsSccBfSwitch:Off, NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • When the NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch option of the NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the NoSrsSccBfSwitch option of the same parameter must also be selected.
  • When the NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch option of the NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: NoSrsSccBfSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB enables beamforming for CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs, increasing the downlink throughput. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB disables beamforming for CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs, and the network performance is not affected.

NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch: If this option is selected, CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs can be paired for MU beamforming, increasing the downlink throughput. If this option is deselected, CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs cannot be paired for MU beamforming, and the network performance is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
SpecUserAlgoSwitch Specified User Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable algorithms related to specified UEs.

WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB allocates PRBs to WBB or MBB UEs based on the preset upper limits of PRB usage. The uplink and downlink upper limits of PRB usage for WBB UEs are specified by the WbbUserUlPrbUpLimit and WbbUserDlPrbUpLimit parameters in the CellWttxParaCfg MO, respectively. The uplink and downlink upper limits of PRB usage for MBB UEs are specified by the MbbUserUlPrbUpLimit and MbbUserDlPrbUpLimit parameters in the CellWttxParaCfg MO, respectively. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not allocate PRBs to WBB or MBB UEs based on the preset upper limits of PRB usage. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the upper limit of PRB usage when scheduling WBB UEs in the downlink. The eNodeB dynamically adjusts the upper limit for WBB UEs based on the values of the DynCtrlUeCntLowPctThld, DynCtrlUeCntHighPctThld, and DynCtrlMbbUePrbThld parameters in the CellWttxParaCfg MO. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not dynamically adjust the upper limit of PRB usage when scheduling WBB UEs in the downlink. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH(WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH), WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH(WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH, WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH:Off, WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • If the DacqSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, the WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH option of the SpecUserAlgoSwitch parameter in the same MO must be deselected.
  • If the WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH option of the SpecUserAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch is selected, the WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH option of the SpecUserAlgoSwitch parameter in the same MO must also be selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
MimoUePaAdaptSw MIMO UE PA Adaptation Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the MIMO UE-specific PA adaptation algorithm. If this parameter is set to ON(On), the PA adaptation algorithm takes effect. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), the algorithm does not take effect. This parameter is mainly used for peak throughput comparison test in LTE FDD. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: Impact in TDD scenarios: If this parameter is set to ON(On), the throughput increases for 4x4 MIMO and 256QAM UEs extremely close to the cell center. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), the throughput of such UEs is not affected.

Impact in FDD scenarios: If this parameter is set to ON, the throughput increases for UEs that are capable of up to 64QAM and are extremely close to the cell center of a single 4x4 MIMO cell not interfered by any neighboring cell. For multiple 4x4 MIMO cells that are interfered by neighboring cells, the throughput for UEs that are capable of up to 64QAM and are extremely close to the cell center decreases. If this parameter is set to OFF, the throughput of UEs that are capable of up to 64QAM and are extremely close to the cell center of a 4x4 MIMO cell is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
NonStandardBwAlgoSw Non-Standard Bandwidth Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable enhanced algorithms in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth.

PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable PCFICH power adaptation in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth. PCFICH power adaptation is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable PDCCH power optimization in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth. PDCCH power optimization is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable adaptive PUCCH and SRS resource allocation in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth. Adaptive PUCCH and SRS resource allocation is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH(PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH), PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH(PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH), PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH(PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH, PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH, PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH:On, PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH:On, PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH:On

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the number of UEs that can be scheduled in each TTI decreases, but the bit error rate of the PCFICH decreases more significantly. As a result, the throughput of CEUs increases. If this option is deselected, the throughput of CEUs decreases.

PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the bit error rate of the PDCCH and the number of UEs that can be scheduled in each TTI can be balanced, increasing the cell throughput. If this option is deselected, the cell throughput decreases.

PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH: When this option is selected, uplink control channel resources are allocated based on the actually available resources of cells with non-standard bandwidths in the event of cell restart. When this option is deselected, uplink control channel resources are allocated based on the available resources of cells with standard bandwidths.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD
CeModeHoSwitch CE Mode Handover Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable switching of coverage enhancement modes. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable coverage-based switching between coverage enhancement modes.

COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable coverage-based switching between NC and CE modes. This option does not apply to eMTC-only or LTE-only cells.

SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable service-based switching of coverage enhancement mode. This option does not apply to eMTC-only or LTE-only cells.

GUI Value Range: COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH(COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH), COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH(COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH), SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH(SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH, COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH, SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH:Off, COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH:Off, SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: When this option is selected, UEs in the cell support switchover between CE mode A and CE mode B based on coverage, decreasing the service drop rate of eMTC UEs far from the cell center and increasing the cell capacity. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: When this option is selected, UEs in the cell support switchover between NC mode and CE mode based on coverage, decreasing the service drop rate of eMTC UEs far from the cell center and increasing the cell capacity. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: When this option is selected, UEs in the cell support switchover between NC mode and CE mode based on services, reducing power consumption of LTE UEs that support CE. When this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
CoordinationAlgoSwitch Coordination Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable coordination algorithms.

MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to optimize multi-user coordination. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW(MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW: If this option is selected, the spectral efficiency improves. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
MeasOptAlgoSwitch Measurement Optimization Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable measurement-related optimization algorithms.

GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW: Indicates whether to cancel inter-frequency measurements after a GSM measurement is triggered when UEs are performing VoIP services. If UEs are performing VoIP services and this option is selected, the eNodeB cancels all inter-frequency measurements when a GSM measurement is triggered. This reduces the GSM measurement reporting delay. If UEs are performing VoIP services and this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not cancel inter-frequency measurements when the GSM measurement is triggered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MEAS_OBJ_PREEMPT_SW: Indicates whether to enable preemption when the number of measurement objects of a UE reaches the specified threshold. If this option is selected and the number of measurement objects of the UE reaches the specified threshold, preemption is enabled to ensure that measurements of algorithms with high priority can be delivered. If this option is deselected and the number of measurement objects of the UE reaches the specified threshold, preemption is disabled and the measurements of algorithms that are triggered later cannot be delivered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CSFB_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW: Indicates whether to terminate inter-frequency measurements after a GSM frequency measurement starts for an E-UTRAN-to-GERAN CSFB process. If this option is selected, the eNodeB terminates all inter-frequency measurements when a GSM frequency measurement is triggered during the CSFB process, reducing the GSM frequency measurement reporting delay. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not terminate inter-frequency measurements when a GSM frequency measurement is triggered during the CSFB process. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW: Indicates whether to recover coverage-based inter-frequency measurement if a GSM measurement is terminated when the GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW option is selected. The eNodeB determines whether to deliver coverage-based inter-frequency measurement again when a GSM measurement is terminated only if the GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

SINR_MEAS_CONFIG_SW: Indicates whether to deliver downlink SINR measurement value to UEs supporting downlink SINR measurement when intra-frequency and inter-frequency periodic MDT and MR measurement events are subscribed to. The eNodeB includes the SINR measurement IE in the measurement configurations to UEs supporting downlink SINR measurement only when this option is selected and intra-frequency and inter-frequency periodic MDT and MR measurement events are subscribed to. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

LOAD_COVERAGE_MEAS_DECOUPLE_SW: Indicates whether to deliver both coverage-based measurement configurations and load-based measurement configurations. If this option is selected, the eNodeB can deliver load-based measurement configurations after it delivers coverage-based measurement configurations. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not deliver load-based measurement configurations after it delivers coverage-based measurement configurations. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CSFB_MEAS_DEL_ACT_PCI_MEAS_SW: Indicates whether the eNodeB deletes the measurement configurations for ECGI reading previously delivered by proactive ANR-based PCI conflict detection when receiving a CSFB indication for a UE. The eNodeB deletes the measurement configuration only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW(Gsm Meas Del Interfreq Switch), MEAS_OBJ_PREEMPT_SW(Meas Obj Preempt Switch), CSFB_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW(Csfb Meas Del Interfreq Switch), GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW(Gsm Meas Recover Interfreq Switch), SINR_MEAS_CONFIG_SW(SINR_MEAS_CONFIG_SW), LOAD_COVERAGE_MEAS_DECOUPLE_SW(Load Coverage Meas Decouple Switch), CSFB_MEAS_DEL_ACT_PCI_MEAS_SW(Active PCI Meas Del During CSFB Meas Sw)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW, MEAS_OBJ_PREEMPT_SW, CSFB_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW, GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW, SINR_MEAS_CONFIG_SW, LOAD_COVERAGE_MEAS_DECOUPLE_SW, CSFB_MEAS_DEL_ACT_PCI_MEAS_SW

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW:Off, MEAS_OBJ_PREEMPT_SW:Off, CSFB_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW:Off, GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW:Off

SINR_MEAS_CONFIG_SW:Off, LOAD_COVERAGE_MEAS_DECOUPLE_SW:On, CSFB_MEAS_DEL_ACT_PCI_MEAS_SW:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW: If the UEs are performing VoIP services and this option is selected, inter-frequency measurements are canceled after a GSM measurement is triggered, reducing the GSM measurement reporting delay and the VoIP call drop rate. If the UEs are performing VoIP services and this option is deselected, inter-frequency measurements are not canceled after a GSM measurement is triggered. In this case, the measurement reporting delay prolongs and the VoIP call drop rate may increase.

MEAS_OBJ_PREEMPT_SW: If this option is selected and the number of measurement objects of a UE reaches the specified threshold, the measurements of features and algorithms that are triggered later can be enabled normally, which improves KPIs. For example, the service drop rate decreases. If this option is deselected and the number of measurement objects reaches the specified threshold, the measurements of features and algorithms that are triggered later cannot be enabled, which deteriorates KPIs. For example, a UE cannot perform coverage-based handovers but can perform blind redirection. As a result, the disconnection delay increases, probably leading to service drops. CSFB_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW: If this option is selected, inter-frequency measurements are terminated after a GSM frequency measurement starts for an E-UTRAN-to-GERAN CSFB process, reducing the GSM frequency measurement reporting delay and the CS fallback delay and improving user experience. If this option is deselected, inter-frequency measurements are not terminated after a GSM frequency measurement starts. In this case, the GSM frequency measurement reporting delay increases and user experience may deteriorate. GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW: If this option is selected, when the signal recovers to the level of terminating GSM measurement, the eNodeB restarts coverage-based inter-frequency measurement according to the signal quality of the serving cell. This allows the UE to be handed over to a non-serving E-UTRA frequency, thereby improving user experience. If this option is deselected, coverage-based inter-frequency measurement is not restarted even if the signal recovers to the level of terminating GSM measurement. In this situation, the UE cannot be handed over to a non-serving E-UTRA frequency, thereby affecting user experience.

LOAD_COVERAGE_MEAS_DECOUPLE_SW: If this option is selected, load balancing takes effect in more scenarios, improving user experience. If the target frequencies of load-based measurement are different from the target frequencies of coverage-based measurement, the interval for the UE to repeat measurements on the target frequencies of coverage-based measurement is prolonged and the interval of reporting coverage-based measurement reports is also prolonged, and this may cause the service drop rate to increase. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not deliver load-based measurement configurations after it delivers coverage-based measurement configurations, affecting inter-carrier load balancing effect and causing user experience to deteriorate.

CSFB_MEAS_DEL_ACT_PCI_MEAS_SW: If this option is selected, the eNodeB deletes the measurement configuration for ECGI reading previously delivered for active PCI conflict detection after the measurement for CSFB starts. The deletion shortens the CSFB measurement reporting delay and therefore shortens the CSFB delay, improving user experience and reducing the probability of falling back to a cell not of the target RAT due to CSFB timeout caused by active PCI conflict detection. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not delete the measurement configuration for ECGI reading, the CSFB measurement reporting delay becomes longer, and user experience may be affected when active PCI conflict detection is enabled.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
FreqLayerSwitch Frequency Layer Switch

Meaning: This parameter includes the following options: UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch, UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch, and UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch. The UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch option takes effect only when the UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch option is selected.

UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch: If this option is selected, UTRAN frequency steering takes effect when measurement configurations are delivered for coverage- and CSFB-based handovers from E-UTRAN to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch: If this option is selected, UTRAN frequency steering takes effect during coverage- and CSFB-based blind handovers from E-UTRAN to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch: If this option is selected, SRVCC flexible steering to UTRAN takes effect during coverage-based SRVCC to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch(UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch), UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch(UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch), UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch(UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch, UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch, UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch:Off, UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch:Off, UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If the UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch option is selected, the UTRAN frequencies delivered for measurement are selected based on handover causes and frequency attributes. This setting reduces the probability that UEs detect certain UTRAN cells during measurements, resulting in a lower handover probability and a higher redirection probability.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
EmimoSwitch eMIMO Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable eMIMO-related algorithms.

Virtual4T4RSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the virtual 4T4R function. This option applies to FDD and TDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected.

EmimoMuMimoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the eMIMO MU-MIMO function. This option applies only to FDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected.

EmimoCpsSwitch: Indicates whether to enable coordinated pilot scheduling for eMIMO. This option applies only to FDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected. If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines the pilot transmission mode based on inter-cell load status.

EmimoFbaSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the following functions: fast beam alignment for eMIMO and extending UE rank reporting period in non-high-speed-railway scenarios when the CQI reporting period is adaptively changed to 40 ms. The preceding two functions are enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

New4TCodebookSwitch: Indicates whether to use the new 4T codebook. The new 4T codebook can be used for UEs that support the new 4T codebook only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the 4TX TM9 MU-MIMO function. This option applies only to FDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected.

DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch: Indicates whether to allow UEs in DRX mode to send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH when SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch is set to ON and CqiMask is set to OFF. This option takes effect when the number of configured downlink transmit antennas of the cell is greater than two. This option applies only to LTE FDD. If this option is selected, UEs in DRX mode can send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH when SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch is set to ON and CqiMask is set to OFF. If this option is deselected, the settings of the SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch and CqiMask parameters determine whether to allow UEs in DRX mode to send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH.

PreciseAmcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable precise AMC. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD. If this option is selected, target IBLERs are adjusted based on the interference level information exchanged with neighboring cells. If this option is deselected, the function does not take effect.

EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CSI reporting enhancement. If this option is selected, CSI reporting enhancement for eMIMO is enabled. A shorter CSI reporting period can be set for UEs running large-packet services and fast beam alignment does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD. If this option is deselected, CSI reporting enhancement for eMIMO is disabled.

PUSCH12Switch: Indicates whether to enable the PUSCH 1-2 feedback mode. This option applies only to LTE FDD. If this option is selected, UEs that support reporting on PUSCH 1-2 use this mode to aperiodically report CQIs. If this option is deselected, the mode cannot be used.

GUI Value Range: Virtual4T4RSwitch(Virtual4T4RSwitch), EmimoMuMimoSwitch(EmimoMuMimoSwitch), EmimoCpsSwitch(EmimoCpsSwitch), EmimoFbaSwitch(EmimoFbaSwitch), New4TCodebookSwitch(New4TCodebookSwitch), 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch(4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch), DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch(DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch), PreciseAmcSwitch(PreciseAmcSwitch), EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch(EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch), PUSCH12Switch(PUSCH12Switch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: Virtual4T4RSwitch, EmimoMuMimoSwitch, EmimoCpsSwitch, EmimoFbaSwitch, New4TCodebookSwitch, 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch, DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch, PreciseAmcSwitch, EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch, PUSCH12Switch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: Virtual4T4RSwitch:Off, EmimoMuMimoSwitch:Off, EmimoCpsSwitch:Off, EmimoFbaSwitch:Off, New4TCodebookSwitch:Off, 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch:Off, DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch:Off, PreciseAmcSwitch:Off, PUSCH12Switch:Off, EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, options of TM9Switch and TM10Switch of EnhMIMOSwitch must be deselected.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch is selected, the DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch must be deselected.
  • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch or Tm4MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, AdptCellEdgePwrAllocSw under the CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch parameter in the same CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch is selected, GLPwrShare must be set to OFF.
  • Only if the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, then the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of Dl256QamAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected.
  • When the PUSCH12Switch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter is selected, the New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter must be deselected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: Virtual4T4RSwitch: If this option is selected, resources can be reused in independent scheduling areas and multi-stream spatial multiplexing can be implemented in overlapping areas. This setting increases the peak cell data rate and the user-perceived data rate in the overlapping areas. If this option is deselected, the virtual 4T4R function does not take effect and common networking is used.

EmimoMuMmoSwitch: If this option is selected, MU-MIMO is used for UEs meeting the pairing requirements for MU-MIMO to increase the spectral efficiency of the cell and user-perceived data rates. If this option is deselected, SU-MIMO is used and MU-MIMO gains cannot be obtained.

EmimoCpsSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines the pilot transmission mode based on inter-cell load status to increase UE throughput. If this option is deselected, the default pilot transmission mode is used and network performance is not affected.

EmimoFbaSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB forms more accurate beams for UEs, increasing the downlink SINR for UEs and user-perceived downlink data rates; however, the uplink throughput may slightly decrease. If this option is deselected, cell performance is not affected.

New4TCodebookSwitch: If this option is selected and close-loop MIMO is used, new 4T codebook is used for UEs that support new 4T codebook and original codebook is used for UEs that do not support new 4T codebook, increasing user-perceived downlink data rates. If this option is deselected and close-loop MIMO is used, original codebook is used for all UEs without affecting the network performance.

4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch: If this option is selected, the cell adaptively determines whether to enter or exit TM9 MU-MIMO mode based on factors such as UE capability, channel condition, and cell load. Then, the cell selects UEs meeting the pairing conditions for 4TX TM9 MU-MIMO scheduling, thereby increasing the cell spectral efficiency and user-perceived rate. After the adaptive TM9 MU-MIMO switch is enabled, the cell adaptively exits TM9 MU-MIMO mode when network conditions are not met. Within the period of determining whether to exit TM9 MU-MIMO mode, negative gains of TM9 SU relative to TM4 SU may be produced for UEs controlled by TM9 MU-MIMO to enter TM9 from TM4. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch: If this option is selected, UEs in DRX mode can send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH if SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch is set to ON and CqiMask is set to OFF. This increases the CQI reporting times so that the CQI can quickly adapt to the channel quality, increasing the UE throughput. However, UE compatibility problem may occur. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

PreciseAmcSwitch: If this option is selected, the downlink data rates of UEs increase in scenarios where interference with neighboring cells fluctuates. However, RBLERs may also increase. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB sets shorter CSI feedback periods for UEs, increasing the reporting accuracy of the downlink CSI for UEs and user-perceived downlink data rates; however, the uplink throughput may slightly decrease. In the case of initial access, the uplink IBLER decreases. If this option is deselected, cell performance is not affected.

PUSCH12Switch: If this option is deselected, the network performance is not affected. If this option is selected, UEs that support reporting on PUSCH 1-2 report subband PMIs in aperiodic CQI reporting, increasing user-perceived downlink data rates. However, the PUSCH resource consumption increases and the uplink data rate slightly decreases.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
RohcSwitch ROHC switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable ROHC in the cell. If this parameter is set to ON, ROHC is enabled for UEs supporting ROHC in the cell. If this parameter is set to OFF, ROHC is disabled in the cell.

The value ON is recommended if VoIP and video services are supported in the cell.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON, ROHC is enabled to reduce header overhead of data packets over the air interface, improving the capacity and the coverage of VoIP and video services.

Applicable Mode: L
McpttSwitch MCPTT Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the guarantee functions related to MCPTT services.

McpttQoSSwitch: Indicates whether to apply GBR to MCPTT services and prioritize scheduling for MCPTT services. GBR is applied to MCPTT services and scheduling is prioritized for MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

McpttCongCtrlSwitch: Indicates whether to enable satisfaction-rate-based admission for MCPTT services. Satisfaction-rate-based admission is enabled for MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

McpttRrcDrxSwitch: Indicates whether to apply a dedicated RRC connection inactivity timer and a dedicated DRX parameter group to MCPTT services. A dedicated RRC connection inactivity timer and a dedicated DRX parameter group are applied to MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

McpttVoiceMgtSwitch: Indicates whether to apply voice optimization guarantee to MCPTT services. Voice optimization guarantee is applied to MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: McpttQoSSwitch(McpttQoSSwitch), McpttCongCtrlSwitch(McpttCongCtrlSwitch), McpttRrcDrxSwitch(McpttRrcDrxSwitch), McpttVoiceMgtSwitch(McpttVoiceMgtSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: McpttQoSSwitch, McpttCongCtrlSwitch, McpttRrcDrxSwitch, McpttVoiceMgtSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: McpttQoSSwitch:OFF, McpttCongCtrlSwitch:OFF, McpttRrcDrxSwitch:OFF, McpttVoiceMgtSwitch:OFF

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch UL SU-MIMO Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable single-user MIMO (SU-MIMO) in the uplink for the cell.

ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch: If this option is deselected, uplink SU MIMO is disabled, and single-codeword scheduling is performed for all UEs. If this option is selected, uplink SU-MIMO is enabled, and the eNodeB performs dual-codeword scheduling for SU-MIMO-capable UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch(ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • The ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both selected.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: For UEs that support uplink SU-MIMO, if the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option is selected, the uplink throughput increases. For UEs that do not support uplink SU-MIMO, the setting of the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option does not affect the uplink throughput.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
HighSpeedInterRatHoSwitch High Speed Inter-RAT Handover Switch

Meaning: Indicates the switches used to optimize inter-RAT handover algorithms for high-speed UEs (UEs moving at a high speed) in high-speed scenarios.

HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch: Indicates whether to enable directional CSFB for high-speed UEs. When this function is enabled, an eNodeB delivers only information about inter-RAT frequencies identified as high-speed or ultra-high-speed frequencies for measurements or blind redirections. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch: Indicates whether to enable directional SRVCC for high-speed UEs. When this function is enabled, an eNodeB delivers only information about inter-RAT frequencies identified as high-speed or ultra-high-speed frequencies for measurements and uses a dedicated time-to-trigger in inter-RAT measurements. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HighSpeedPsDirSwitch: Indicates whether to enable directional PS handover for high-speed UEs. When this function is enabled, an eNodeB delivers only information about inter-RAT frequencies identified as high-speed or ultra-high-speed frequencies for measurements and uses a dedicated time-to-trigger in inter-RAT measurements. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch(HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch), HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch(HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch), HighSpeedPsDirSwitch(HighSpeedPsDirSwitch)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch, HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch, HighSpeedPsDirSwitch

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch:Off, HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch:Off, HighSpeedPsDirSwitch:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No

Impact on Radio Network Performance: HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch: When this option is selected, high-speed UEs can be handed over to dedicated UTRAN frequencies through CSFB, increasing the CSFB success rate for high-speed UEs. When this option is deselected, high-speed UEs and low-speed UEs (UEs moving at a low speed) are treated in the same way in CSFB, affecting CSFB performance.

HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch: When this option is selected, high-speed UEs can be handed over to dedicated UTRAN frequencies through SRVCC in a more timely manner, increasing the SRVCC success rate for high-speed UEs. When this option is deselected, high-speed UEs and low-speed UEs are treated in the same way in SRVCC, affecting SRVCC performance.

HighSpeedPsDirSwitch: When this option is selected, high-speed UEs can be handed over to dedicated UTRAN frequencies through PS handovers and redirections in a more timely manner, increasing the PS handover and redirection success rates for high-speed UEs. When this option is deselected, high-speed UEs and low-speed UEs are treated in the same way in PS handovers and redirections, affecting PS handover and redirection performance.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
EmcSpsSchSwitch EMC Semi-Persistent Scheduling Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to use semi-persistent scheduling during talk spurts of emergency calls. If this parameter is set to OFF, dynamic scheduling is used. When this parameter is set to ON and the SpsSchSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter is selected, uplink semi-persistent scheduling is used for an emergency call if the emergency call meets the conditions for activating uplink semi-persistent scheduling. When this parameter is set to ON and the SpsSchSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter is selected, downlink semi-persistent scheduling is used for an emergency call if the emergency call meets the conditions for activating downlink semi-persistent scheduling. The use of semi-persistent scheduling reduces PDCCH resource consumption. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: When this parameter is set to ON, the use of semi-persistent scheduling for emergency calls reduces PDCCH resource consumption and CQI reporting overhead, increasing the emergency call capacity in emergencies. When this parameter is set to OFF, PDCCH resource consumption is high and the emergency call capacity is low; however, each emergency call can experience better service quality.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
UlIcsRbRatio UL ICS RB Ratio

Meaning: Indicates the maximum proportion of RBs that can be used for jointly scheduling in the uplink. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: 1~100

Unit: %

Actual Value Range: 1~100

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: 62

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to a large value, a large number of RBs can be used for joint scheduling in the uplink, improving the performance of joint-scheduling UEs. In this case, a small number of RBs are allocated to non-joint-scheduling UEs. If this parameter is set to a small value, a small number of RBs can be used for joint scheduling in the uplink, degrading the performance of joint-scheduling UEs. In this case, a large number of RBs are allocated to non-joint-scheduling UEs.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
UlIcsVoLTEPLTh VoLTE Path Loss Threshold for UL ICS

Meaning: Indicates the threshold of path loss for selecting VoLTE UEs to preferentially perform uplink joint scheduling. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: 50~150

Unit: dB

Actual Value Range: 50~150

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: 115

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to a large value, a small number of VoLTE UEs are selected for UL ICS. If this parameter is set to a small value, a large number of VoLTE UEs are selected for UL ICS.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
LbsSwitch LBS Feature Switch

Meaning: This parameter is intended for the LTE cellular positioning engine feature of the service anchor.

TDOA_SWITCH: Indicates whether TDOA-based positioning is enabled. The TDoA feature takes effect only when this option is selected. The TDoA feature applies only to LampSite eNodeBs with a bandwidth of 20 MHz or 15 MHz. When this option is selected, physical cells cannot be set up on the LBBP, UBBPda, UBBPdb, or UBBPea board. Otherwise, physical cells cannot be activated. This feature must work with pRRU3911. When the CellCapacityMode parameter of an FDD cell is set to LARGECAPACITY, TDoA-based positioning is not supported. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: TDOA_SWITCH(TDOA_SWITCH)

Actual Value Range: TDOA_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: TDOA_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the same CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the RruUeMapSwitch parameter in the same CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF.

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
DtxDetectionAlgoSwitch DTX Detection Algo Switch

Meaning: BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW: Indicates whether to disable DTX identification when NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode. If this option is selected and NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode, DTX identification is disabled. If this option is deselected and NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode, DTX identification is enabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW(BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW: If this option is deselected and NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode, DTX identification is enabled, lowering the residual packet error rate and improving user experience. However, DTX identification for a large number of UEs leads to a high probability that CCE allocation for the cell is restricted and the cell throughput decreases. If this option is selected and NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode, DTX identification is disabled, increasing the residual packet error rate and degrading user experience.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
SasAlgorithmSwitch SAS Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable the spectrum access system (SAS) algorithm.

SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW: Indicates whether to use the SAS to perform dynamic spectrum management for the base station. If this option is selected, SAS-based dynamic spectrum management takes effect, and the MAE applies for spectrum to the SAS server and automatically configures cell parameters, including frequency, bandwidth, and transmit power, based on the application result. If this option is deselected, SAS-based dynamic spectrum management does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW(SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: Yes

Impact on Radio Network Performance: SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW: If this option is selected, the SAS dynamically manages the cell parameters such as spectrum bandwidth and transmit power. If an excessively small bandwidth or low transmit power is allocated to the cell, the cell coverage and throughput decrease. If this option is deselected, dynamic spectrum management does not take effect, and cell performance is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L-TDD
DacqSwitch DACQ Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable dynamic AMBR control by QCI (DACQ). If this parameter is set to ON(On), DACQ is enabled. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), DACQ is disabled. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

  • If the EnhancementCtrlSwitch option of the DacqEnhancementSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the DacqSwitch parameter in the same MO must be set to ON.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON(On), DACQ is enabled. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), DACQ is disabled.

UlHighOrderCompSwitch UL High Order CoMP Switch

UuBasedV2xAlgoSwitch Uu-based V2X Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates the Uu-based V2X service algorithm switch, which specifies whether to enable algorithms related to Uu-based V2X services. UU_BASED_V2X_SCH_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable dedicated scheduling for Uu-based V2X services. Dedicated scheduling for Uu-based V2X services is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: UU_BASED_V2X_SCH_SWITCH(UU_BASED_V2X_SCH_SWITCH)

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: UU_BASED_V2X_SCH_SWITCH:Off
