Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: UU_BASED_V2X_SCH_SWITCH: If this option is selected when there is a large number of MBB services, V2X services are preferentially scheduled when compared with other low-priority MBB services. Therefore, the end-to-end delay and packet loss rate of V2X services decrease but the delay and packet loss rate of other low-priority services increase. If this option is deselected when there is a large number of MBB services, the end-to-end delay and packet loss rate of V2X services increase.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
DlSchExtSwitch DL Scheduling Extension Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable downlink scheduling algorithms for a cell. This parameter is an extension of the DlSchSwitch parameter and includes the following options:

CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to restrict the amount of air interface resources allocated to UEs running video services when congestion occurs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB restricts air interface resources allocated to UEs running video services when congestion occurs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not restrict air interface resources allocated to video UEs when congestion occurs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to select the MCS index based on the TBS for PTT voice services (with QCI 65, QCI 66, or enhanced extended QCIs). The MCS index is selected based on the TBS in downlink dynamic scheduling for PTT voice services only if this option is selected.

DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH: Indicates whether to adopt the service-based time-differentiated scheduling policy in the downlink. If this option is selected, the eNodeB performs the service-based time-differentiated scheduling policy in the downlink. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform the service-based time-differentiated scheduling policy in the downlink. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to support the scheduling of transport blocks with TBS indexes of 26A and 33A for TM9 and TM10 UEs. The TBS indexes of 26A and 33A are supported only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable downlink AMBR optimization. If this option is selected, the eNodeB more accurately restricts the scheduling of the last packet for UEs based on AMBRs. If this option is deselected, the optimization does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW: Indicates whether the eNodeB estimates PDCP SNs at the UE side in immediate reestablishment after handovers. If this option is selected, the eNodeB estimates PDCP SNs at the UE side. The eNodeB adds an estimation amount to the minimum SN among the SNs of the PDCP packets for which an ACK is not received. The new SN is used as the starting SN after the reestablishment. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not estimate PDCP SNs received at the UE side. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DL_COORD_SCH_EXP_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to enable coordinating scheduling experience optimization for DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO. If this option is selected, coordinating scheduling experience optimization for DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO is enabled. If this option is deselected, coordinating scheduling experience optimization for DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO is disabled. The setting of this option takes effect only if DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO are enabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW: Indicates whether to ensure the best-effort GBR for UEs with a specific QCI or SPID. If this option is selected, the eNodeB ensures the best-effort GBR for UEs with a specific QCI or SPID. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not ensure the best-effort GBR for UEs with a specific QCI or SPID. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

SMART_FSS_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable smart frequency selective scheduling. If the FreqSelSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter and the SMART_FSS_SWITCH option are both selected, smart frequency selective scheduling takes effect. The eNodeB dynamically adjusts the period of triggering aperiodic CQI reporting from UEs involved in frequency selective scheduling and also adjusts the validity period of aperiodically reported CQIs. Therefore, the settings of the FSUEAperCQITrigPeriod and FSUESbCQIValidityPeriod parameters are invalid in smart frequency selective scheduling. If the SMART_FSS_SWITCH option is deselected, the settings of the FSUEAperCQITrigPeriod and FSUESbCQIValidityPeriod parameters still take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PUSCH_1X_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the PUSCH 1-0 and PUSCH 1-1 feedback modes. These modes can be used only for short-TTI UEs. If this option is selected, UEs that support reporting on PUSCH 1-0 or PUSCH 1-1 use the corresponding mode to aperiodically report CSI. If this option is deselected, the PUSCH 1-0 and PUSCH 1-1 feedback modes cannot be used. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH(CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH), PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH(PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH), DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH(DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH), MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH(MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH), DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH(DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH), SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW(SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW), DL_COORD_SCH_EXP_OPT_SW(DL_COORD_SCH_EXP_OPT_SW), BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW(BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW), SMART_FSS_SWITCH(SMART_FSS_SWITCH), PUSCH_1X_SWITCH(PUSCH_1X_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH, PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH, DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH, MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH, DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH, SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW, DL_COORD_SCH_EXP_OPT_SW, BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW, SMART_FSS_SWITCH, PUSCH_1X_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH:Off, PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH:Off, DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH:Off, MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH:Off, DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH:Off, DL_COORD_SCH_EXP_OPT_SW:Off, SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW:Off, BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW:Off, SMART_FSS_SWITCH:Off, PUSCH_1X_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH: If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected. If this option is selected, the eNodeB restricts the amount of resources allocated to UEs running high-rate video services in heavy-load scenarios to improve the experience of other services such as web browsing. However, video experience is affected.

PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the number of HARQ retransmissions for downlink PTT voice services (with QCI 65, QCI 66, or an enhanced extended QCI) and the IBLER decrease.

DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH: If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected. If this option is selected, the average UE throughput increases when the network is moderately or heavily loaded.

MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the data rates of TM9 and TM10 UEs increase in the cell center. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, AMBR-based scheduling restriction is more accurate but the user-perceived downlink rate decreases when there are UEs with limited AMBRs. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW: If this option is deselected, the PDCP SNs at the eNodeB side may be smaller than the PDCP SNs at the UE side. After a reestablishment, certain data sent to the UE will be discarded. As a result, the UE throughput decreases. If this option is selected, the eNodeB increases the eNodeB PDCP SNs based on the PDCP SN estimation result after a reestablishment. The PDCP SN inconsistency between the eNodeB and UE is relieved. However, the sudden change in PDCP SNs after the reestablishment may cause UE incompatibility.

SMART_FSS_SWITCH: If this option and the FreqSelSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter are both selected, smart frequency selective scheduling is enabled. The eNodeB dynamically adjusts the period of triggering aperiodic CQI reporting from UEs involved in frequency selective scheduling and also adjusts the validity period of aperiodically reported CQIs. This reduces uplink overheads, improving downlink performance in multi-UE heavy-load scenarios. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

PUSCH_1X_SWITCH: If this option is selected, UEs that support reporting on PUSCH 1-0 or PUSCH 1-1 report only wideband information during aperiodic CSI reporting, which reduces the PUSCH overheads. If this option is deselected, network performance is not affected.

Applicable Mode: L
MPMUDetectSwitch MPMU Fault Detection Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable MPMU-level fault detection.

PRRUDETECT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the system starts to record the positions of the UE and MPMU and saves the positions in the maintenance CHR log. If this option is deselected, the system stops recording such information.

GUI Value Range: PRRUDETECT_SWITCH(PRRUDETECT_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: PRRUDETECT_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: PRRUDETECT_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: None

Applicable Mode: L
VmsSwitch Vertical Multiple Sectors Algorithm Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable algorithms related to vertical multiple sectors (VMS) or massive MIMO (32T32R) cell split.

PrbBalanceSw: If this option is selected in VMS scenarios, load balancing between inner and outer cells is enabled. If this option is deselected in VMS scenarios, load balancing between inner and outer cells is disabled. If this option is selected in massive MIMO (32T32R) scenarios, load balancing between cells served by the same AAU is enabled. If this option is deselected in massive MIMO (32T32R) scenarios, load balancing between cells served by the same AAU is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: PrbBalanceSw(PrbBalanceSw)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: PrbBalanceSw

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: PrbBalanceSw:Off

Parameter Relationship:

-Configuration constraints:

  • Options PrbBalanceSw of VmsSwitch and IntraFreqMroSwitch of MroSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch cannot be selected simultaneously.

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: PrbBalanceSw: In VMS scenarios, if this option is selected and the load of the outer cell is heavier than the load of the inner cell, the user-perceived data rate of UEs in the outer cell increases and the capacity of the inner cell increases. However, the number of handovers increases, and the handover success rate slightly decreases. In massive MIMO (32T32R) scenarios, if this option is selected and the load difference between intra-frequency cells is large, the user-perceived data rate of UEs in the heavily loaded cell increases and the capacity of the lightly loaded cell increases. However, the number of handovers increases, and the handover success rate slightly decreases.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD
RrcReestDataFwdSwitch RRC Reestablishment Data Forwarding Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether the eNodeB serving the cell forwards data that has not been sent to a UE in the cell (as the source cell) to the target cell when the UE experiences an RRC connection reestablishment so that the target cell can send the data to the UE. If this parameter is set to ON(On), the eNodeB forwards the data to the target cell and the target cell sends the data to the UE after the RRC connection is reestablished in the target cell. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), the eNodeB discards the data in the preceding scenario. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(Off), ON(On)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(Off)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: If this parameter is set to ON, the probability of TCP/UDP packet loss due to RRC connection reestablishments decreases. Setting this parameter to OFF results in the opposite effect. If this parameter is set to ON for a cell in which the RRC connections of more than 2% of the served UEs are reestablished, the bit error rate (BER), RLC retransmission rate, and service drop rate increase, and the success rates of RRC connection setup and reestablishment decrease.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD
SmallBandOptSwitch Small Bandwidth Optimization Switch

Meaning: Indicates the small bandwidth optimization switch, which is used to control the optimization features of small-bandwidth cells, such as 1.4 MHz, 3 MHz, and 5 MHz cell.

SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB configures different heavy-UE number thresholds depending on the cell bandwidth configuration and performs optimization according to the settings of RlcParaAdaptSwitch and SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch. Timer parameters related to the RlcParaAdaptSwitch optimization are set to the fixed value of 200 ms. Timer parameters related to the SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch optimization are dependent on the GUI values. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB performs RLC optimization depending on the GUI values of the RlcParaAdaptSwitch and SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch parameters. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

GUI Value Range: SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH(SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH:Off

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB configures different heavy-UE number thresholds depending on the cell bandwidth configuration. This helps increase the downlink user-perceived data rate or capacity. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB performs RLC optimization depending on the GUI values of the RlcParaAdaptSwitch and SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch parameters. It is recommended that this option be selected when the load is heavy in small-bandwidth scenarios (1.4 MHz, 3 MHz, or 5 MHz cell) and the ARQ retransmission rate is high. It is recommended that this option be deselected when CCE resources are insufficient in small-bandwidth scenarios and the PRB usage exceeds 95%. If this option is selected, the downlink ARQ retransmission rate, CCE usage, and UE scheduling number decrease, and the uplink CCE usage may increase.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD
IdleModeEdrxSwitch Idle Mode Extended DRX Switch

Meaning: Indicates whether to enable idle mode eDRX for LTE UEs. For details about idle mode eDRX, see 3GPP TS 23.682. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GUI Value Range: OFF(OFF), ON(ON)

Unit: None

Actual Value Range: OFF, ON

MML Default Value: None

Recommended Value: OFF(OFF)

Parameter Relationship: None

Service Interrupted After Modification: No (And no impact on the UE in idle mode)

Impact on Radio Network Performance: When this parameter is set to ON, power consumption for UEs to monitor paging messages decreases, thereby extending the battery life of UEs. However, the timeliness of paging responses deteriorates. Setting this parameter to OFF does not produce the preceding effects.

Applicable Mode: L-FDD, L-TDD

Example

Example 1: To modify the settings of cell-level algorithm switches with the parameters as follows: Local cell ID: 0, Dynamic adjust voltage algorithm switch: DynAdjVoltSwitch-1, run the following command: 
MOD CELLALGOSWITCH: LocalCellId=0, DynAdjVoltSwitch=DynAdjVoltSwitch-1;
Example 2: To modify the settings of cell-level algorithm switches with the parameters as follows: Local cell ID: 0, PUCCH algorithm switch: PucchSwitch-1, run the following command:
MOD CELLALGOSWITCH: LocalCellId=0, PucchAlgoSwitch=PucchSwitch-1;
